----- BUILD BRIEF -----
# Build brief: optional REST-polling Binance SPOT source (`TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT=rest`)

Survey of `Terminal-L1-L2-development-indicator-sdk` (main), 2026-10-26. Paths are relative to `apps/runtime/src/` unless stated. The repository was not modified.

## 0. Step one, independent of the REST work: spot WebSocket URL override

Binance runs an un-geo-blocked spot stream mirror, `wss://data-stream.binance.vision:9443`. Today the spot WS origin is the constant `BINANCE_SPOT_WS_BASE_URL` (`providers/binance/binance-config.js:4`), read by `wsBaseUrlsFor` (:121-132), frozen into the market-kind context (:134-146) and made the route URL `${base}/stream` in `binance-connection.js:108-110, 291-322`. The REST origin is the constant at :7, read by `restBaseUrlFor` (:111-119) and used by `binance-http-client.js:261, 292, 314`.

Build:
1. `binance-config.js`: add `binanceEndpointsFromEnv(env)` returning `{ spotWsBaseUrl, spotRestBaseUrl }` with the current constants as defaults; `wsBaseUrlsFor(marketKind, endpoints)` and `restBaseUrlFor(marketKind, endpoints)` accept the override for SPOT only. PERPETUAL constants (:5-6, :8) untouched.
2. `binance-connection.js:112-139`: new option `endpoints`, passed to `createBinanceMarketKindContext` (:226-229) and as `baseUrl` to `fetchDepthSnapshot`/`createBinanceKlineBackfill` (:251, :1612, :1652); `binance-http-client.js` functions take an optional `baseUrl`. `server/app.js:128` passes `endpoints: binanceEndpointsFromEnv(process.env)`.
3. `platform/config-registry.js`: `TT_BINANCE_SPOT_WS_BASE_URL` (new kind `wsOrigin`: bare `ws:`/`wss:` origin, port allowed; `describeExpected`/`valueIsValid` :261-337) and `TT_BINANCE_SPOT_REST_BASE_URL` (kind `origin`), both `[TERMINAL_C]`, `.env`. Matching rows in `docs/operations/09-configuration.md` (:35ff; `scripts/validate-docs.cjs:91-102` enforces them).
4. Tests: `test/providers/binance/binance-config.test.js:53-70` gains the override cases; `binance-connection.test.js:128-142` gains "with `endpoints.spotWsBaseUrl` the SPOT route URL is `<override>/stream` and the two PERPETUAL URLs are unchanged"; `binance-http-client.test.js:35-41` gains the REST override URL.

This alone lets a VPS in a blocked region run the normal stream transport for spot. It does not help this container (the proxy refuses WebSocket upgrades); hence the REST source.

## 1. Integration point

The seam is the connection object, which `market-data-server.js` duck-types: `onMessage` (:3602), `connect`/`getStatus` (:3609-3610; `requiredRoutesConnected` :3533-3539 needs `status.SPOT.public === "CONNECTED"`), `subscribeMany`/`unsubscribeMany` (:1261, :1288), `getFeedHealth` (:822, :955, :2680), `getOrderBook`/`getOrderBookStatus` (:1018, :1130-1137), `recoverStaleSubscription` (:2642), `fetchRecentKlines` (:1807), `disconnect` (:3629). The public surface is `binance-connection.js:1930-1949`.

Build `providers/binance/binance-rest-connection.js` exporting `createBinanceRestConnection(options)` with that surface, selected in `server/app.js:128`:

```js
const connection = transport === "rest"
  ? createBinanceRestConnection({ telemetry, fetchImpl, endpoints, pollMs, weightBudget })
  : createBinanceConnection({ telemetry, webSocketImpl, fetchImpl, endpoints, backpressureMaxQueueDepth });
```

It reuses unchanged: `binance-config.js`, `binance-subscription-guard.js` (:99-115), `providers/shared/subscription-registry.js`, `binance-event-normalizer.js` (`normalizeBinanceEvent` :671-690, fed raw objects in stream shape so the output is the same frozen envelope :540-552), `binance-feed-health.js`, `binance-kline-backfill.js`; plus `binance-book-assembler.js` with one additive method (section 3) and `binance-http-client.js` with `fetchAggTrades`. It emits envelopes as the WS path does: `patchEnvelopeHealthContext` (`binance-connection.js:1446-1461`) and `feedHealth.recordEvent` before emission (:1356-1358).

Files to touch: new `binance-rest-connection.js`, `binance-rest-trade-pager.js`, `binance-rest-weight-budget.js`; `binance-http-client.js` (`fetchAggTrades`, `baseUrl`; it already returns `headers` :248); `binance-book-assembler.js` (one method); `binance-config.js`, `server/app.js:128`, `config-registry.js`, `09-configuration.md`; `market-data-server.js:845-856` only to carry the health context's `reason` into `healthFrame` (today only `WIRE_VALUE_OUT_OF_RANGE` does, :839-843).

Must not change: `binance-connection.js` beyond the `endpoints` option, `binance-event-normalizer.js`, `binance-feed-health.js`, `binance-backpressure-queue.js`, `server/frames.js`, `provider-router.js`, every `indicators/**`, the web app.

## 2. Configuration (`config-registry.js`, `[TERMINAL_C]`, `.env`)

| Setting | Kind | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| `TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT` | enum `ws`,`rest` | `ws` | absent or `ws` is byte-for-byte today's path |
| `TT_BINANCE_SPOT_WS_BASE_URL` | wsOrigin | `wss://stream.binance.com` | documented alternative `wss://data-stream.binance.vision:9443` |
| `TT_BINANCE_SPOT_REST_BASE_URL` | origin | `https://api.binance.com` | documented alternative `https://data-api.binance.vision` |
| `TT_BINANCE_REST_POLL_MS` | number, integer, min 250 | `1000` | trades and klines cadence |
| `TT_BINANCE_REST_WEIGHT_BUDGET` | number, integer, 100..6000 | `4000` | REQUEST_WEIGHT the runtime allows itself per minute |

`09-configuration.md` gets the rows plus a paragraph: spot only, perpetual pairs unselectable, container prerequisite `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1` (section 5). `bootProcess` (`platform/process-env.js:48-67`) validates them at start.

## 3. Polling design per family

**Trades (`/api/v3/aggTrades`, weight 4).** The WS path emits one envelope per `@trade` message (`binance-connection.js:76`), normalized from keys `t,p,q,T,m` (`normalizer :21, :42, :318-325`, event time `T` :297), and downstream derives side as `isBuyerMaker ? "sell" : "buy"` (`frames.js:163`, `l2-memory/live-binding.js:52`) and dedupes by `trade_id` (`server/history.js:206-214`). Pager: on subscribe, `GET aggTrades?symbol&limit=1` sets `cursor = a`. Each tick: `GET aggTrades?symbol&fromId=cursor+1&limit=1000`; while a page is full, fetch the next at once (cap 5 pages per tick, then `feedHealth.beginRecovery(TRADE)` and continue next tick, never drop). Aggregate ids are contiguous: a row with `a <= cursor` is dropped as a duplicate; a jump is telemetry `market_data.binance_rest.trade_id_gap`, never filled. Each row becomes raw `{e:"trade", E: receivedAtMs, s, t: a, p, q, T, m, M}` for `normalizeBinanceEvent`: `payload.tradeId` is the aggregate id, `eventTime` the exchange time `T`, emission order `a` ascending.

**Bars (`/api/v3/klines`, weight 1 for limit ≤ 100).** Today 1m bars come from the `@kline_1m` stream, not from trades (`market-data-server.js:119, :1248-1249`; `klineEnvelopeToFrame` `frames.js:168-210`, closed from `k.x`); backfill uses REST rows (`restKlineRowToBarFrame` `frames.js:212-263`). Each tick: `GET klines?symbol&interval=1m&startTime=<previous minute open>&limit=2`. Row 0 is emitted `x:true` once per `openTime`, only after `closeTime < receivedAtMs`; row 1 `x:false` whenever `c,v,n` changed. Raw: `{e:"kline", E: receivedAtMs, s, k:{t,T,s,i:"1m",o,c,h,l,v,n,x,q,V,Q}}`; `f`,`L`,`B` are optional (`normalizer :24, :48-64`). `fetchRecentKlines` delegates to the existing backfill.

**Depth (`/api/v3/depth?limit=1000`, weight 50) as periodic snapshots.** The assembler needs a snapshot and then a bridging diff: `applySnapshotStatus` sets `needsBridgeCheck = true` (`binance-book-assembler.js:352`) and `getStatus` stays `BUFFERING` until a diff with `U <= lastUpdateId+1` arrives (:85-105, :264-288, :381-398). A snapshot-only feed never leaves BUFFERING, so add one explicit method, `applyStandaloneSnapshotStatus(body)`: the same validation (`snapshotDepthBody` :27-43, crossed book :339-344, level cap), then `needsBridgeCheck = false`, `localUpdateId = body.lastUpdateId`, buffer cleared. No synthetic update ids. The transport keeps one real assembler per pair and feeds it every poll, so `getOrderBook` returns a READY book whose `lastUpdateId` is the snapshot's own id (`emitBook` fingerprints on it, `market-data-server.js:963-968`).

For the L2 engine the transport emits one DEPTH envelope per snapshot: `firstUpdateId = lastUpdateId = snapshot.lastUpdateId`, `previousUpdateId: null` (SPOT, `normalizer :364-367`, `assembler :69-71`) and `bids`/`asks` = the observed level changes between the previous and current snapshot, computed only inside the price window both snapshots cover (bids `>= max(oldLowestBid, newLowestBid)`, asks mirrored); a vanished level is reported with quantity `"0"` as Binance does; levels that merely left the 1000-level window are not reported. `live-binding.js:44-47` makes a `book` frame whose `delta` is those changes; `memory-engine.js:201-250` applies it as a diff while `live`, seeds on the first READY book (:131-141), resyncs after a gap (:143-158), and `coverageOf` (:106-114) reads the real edge of the 1000-level book. A failed poll emits nothing; `429/418` or three consecutive failures mark DEPTH recovering, the `gateBookState` equivalent returns `UNAVAILABLE: RECOVERY_IN_PROGRESS` and `live-binding.js:45` sends a `gap` frame, as a WS sequence gap does (`binance-connection.js:1377-1390`). The periodic and shadow refreshes (:65, :867-886, :1580-1641) do not apply.

## 4. Honesty

- `getStatus()` reports `SPOT.public` `CONNECTED` only after a successful `exchangeInfo?symbol=BTCUSDT` probe (weight 20, `fetchExchangeInfo` :303-319), `DISCONNECTED` after three consecutive failures; PERPETUAL routes stay `DISCONNECTED`, so a perpetual `subscribeMany` throws today's "route is connected" error (`binance-connection.js:1004-1016`) and `requestSwitch` parks the selection (`market-data-server.js:3496-3508`).
- `getFeedHealth()` returns the feed-health context plus `transport: "rest"`, `pollMs`, `lastPollLatencyMs` and `reason`: `REST_POLL` while READY, `REST_RATE_LIMITED`, `REST_POLL_FAILED`, `REST_BACKOFF_<ms>` while degraded. `market-data-server.js:845-856` passes the worst context's `reason` to `healthFrame` (`frames.js:663-694` accepts a reason with READY, limit 256 chars :28); the browser already prints it (`apps/web/src/components/chart/MainPanel.tsx:2436`). The health fingerprint includes it (`market-data-server.js:944`), so every change is broadcast.
- Staleness keeps today's thresholds (`binance-feed-health.js:7-12`: TRADE 30 s, KLINE 10 s, DEPTH 5 s). A successful empty trade poll is a real observation and records `recordEvent(TRADE)`; a failed poll records nothing, so silence becomes `stale: true` as with a dead stream. No envelope is ever emitted without a row.
- Backoff: on `RATE_LIMITED` (`binance-http-client.js:231-234` parses `Retry-After`) every family of every pair pauses until the later of `Retry-After` and exponential backoff (1 s base, 30 s cap, 20% jitter, as `binance-connection.js:47-49, :1704-1740`), with `beginRecovery` on all families and telemetry `market_data.binance_rest.backoff`. A `418` (IP ban) waits the full `Retry-After` and sets `DISCONNECTED`.
- Weight accounting: every response's `x-mbx-used-weight-1m` (already in `result.value.headers`) is authoritative; the budget module also projects per calendar minute: aggTrades 4 per page, klines 1, depth 50 (limit 1000), exchangeInfo 20, kline backfill 5 per 1000-row page. With one pair at `pollMs=1000`: 60·(4+1) + 60·50 = 3300/min ≤ 4000, so depth polls every tick. The scheduler stretches the depth cadence first (to the smallest multiple of `pollMs` that fits), then klines, never trades; two pairs: 600 + 3000 (depth every 2 s) = 3600. If the header reaches the budget, all polling stops until the minute boundary; the hard limit 6000 is never approached (margin ≥ 2000).

## 5. Proxy support

The REST client is Node's built-in `fetch` (`binance-http-client.js:208-211`, `fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch` :253; `binance-connection.js:116`), not `undici` or `https`; `redirect: "error"`, 5 s timeout, 2 MB cap. It ignores `HTTPS_PROXY` unless `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1` (Node ≥ 22.21; the repo pins 24.21.0, `package.json:6-7`). CA: Node reads `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`, which this container sets beside `SSL_CERT_FILE` (Node ignores the latter). No code change: document both in `09-configuration.md` and `03-server-runbook.md`; on the owner's machine neither is set and nothing changes. `ws` never honoured a proxy.

## 6. Tests (jest, `apps/runtime/test/providers/binance/`)

Fixture: `test/fixtures/binance-rest/fake-binance-server.js`, a `node:http` server on `127.0.0.1:0` (precedent `test/fixtures/offline-serving-process.js`) serving scripted `aggTrades`, `klines`, `depth`, `exchangeInfo` bodies with `x-mbx-used-weight-1m`, status overrides (`429` plus `Retry-After`, `418`, `500`) and a request log; the connection gets `endpoints.spotRestBaseUrl = http://127.0.0.1:<port>`. Unit tests use `jest.fn` fetch fakes as in `binance-connection.test.js:1608-1628`.

- `binance-rest-trade-pager.test.js`: 2500 trades over three pages arrive once, in order, no id missing; overlapping pages drop duplicates by id; a jump logs telemetry and fabricates nothing; the 5-page cap marks TRADE recovering.
- `binance-rest-weight-budget.test.js`: per-family ledger, depth cadence stretching, hard stop at the header, minute-boundary reset (fake clock).
- `binance-rest-connection.test.js`: `getStatus` lifecycle; backoff on 429/418 honouring `Retry-After`; failed polls emit nothing and turn `stale`; health context carries `transport`, `reason`, latency; perpetual subscribe throws; `quiesce` drains in-flight polls.
- `binance-book-assembler.test.js`: `applyStandaloneSnapshotStatus` reaches READY with the snapshot's `lastUpdateId`, rejects malformed and crossed books; the real assembler plus `live-binding.binanceDepthFrame` feed `memory-engine` (seed, diffs, gap, resync; `stats.resyncs` counts one per gap, not per poll).
- `binance-rest-shape-parity.test.js`: a recorded fixture of WS `trade`/`kline`/`depthUpdate` messages and the equivalent REST rows for the same instant; per family the normalized envelopes have identical key sets and types, identical `provider`, `venue`, `marketKind`, `eventType`, `timingClass`, and identical `price`, `quantity`, `isBuyerMaker`, `eventTime` per print; the documented differences (`tradeId` space, kline `eventTime` = poll time, depth `firstUpdateId === lastUpdateId`) are asserted.
- `test/server/app.test.js:210-230`: with `TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT=rest` `createApp` constructs no WebSocket; absent, `createBinanceConnection` is used.
- `test/platform/config-registry.test.js` covers the new entries automatically (:46-60).

## 7. Acceptance criteria

1. In this container, `TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT=rest TT_BINANCE_SPOT_REST_BASE_URL=https://data-api.binance.vision NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1 node apps/runtime/scripts/serve/run-ws-server.js 8001 BTCUSDT` reaches `CONNECTED`, and the web app shows BTC spot trades with side, a forming and a closing 1m bar, a READY book, and a health line carrying `REST_POLL`.
2. `x-mbx-used-weight-1m` observed over ten minutes stays ≤ 4000.
3. `npm test` in `apps/runtime` passes in full; `npm run docs:check` passes.
4. With the setting absent the WS path is untouched: `binance-connection.test.js` (3805 lines) passes unmodified apart from the added URL-override case, and `app.test.js:226` still finds the `stream.binance.com/stream` socket.

## 8. Risks and what is not supported

- Not supported: perpetuals (no mirror; routes stay DISCONNECTED); sub-second latency (trades arrive up to `pollMs` plus round trip late; the engine's late-trade rule, `memory-engine.js:83-94`, keeps order); order-book diffs (a 1000-level snapshot at most once per tick; iceberg and exhaustion engines see coarser deltas, to be documented as degraded on REST); bookTicker; switching transport at runtime (aggregate and raw trade ids differ).
- Risks: endpoint weights may change (the header is authoritative, the ledger a projection); the mirror's lag behind the primary is undocumented (measure `T` versus `receivedAt` in the acceptance run); a 1000-level book is shallower than the 5000-level WS snapshot, so lane-0 heatmap coverage (±5%) may be partial on volatile days, with `coverageOf` marking the real edge.

## Summary

1. First step: `TT_BINANCE_SPOT_WS_BASE_URL` override (config.js:4,121-146; connection.js:291-322) with registry entry, doc row and one URL test; helps a VPS, not this container.
2. The REST source is a second connection factory with the surface of `binance-connection.js:1930-1949`, chosen in `app.js:128`.
3. Normalizer, feed health, frames, L2 engine, router and web app stay unchanged; raw objects are built in stream shape, so envelopes are canonical.
4. Trades: aggTrades `fromId` paging, dedupe by id, side from `isBuyerMaker`, order preserved, gaps reported not filled.
5. Bars: klines `limit=2`, previous bar emitted closed once, forming bar on change; matches today's kline-stream path.
6. Depth: 1000-level snapshots into the real assembler via one additive method; L2 gets observed level changes with `U === u === lastUpdateId`, gap and resync frames as today.
7. Health reason `REST_POLL`/`REST_RATE_LIMITED`/`REST_POLL_FAILED` reaches the browser; empty polls are observations, failed polls are silence.
8. Weight ledger plus the exchange header, budget 4000/min by default, depth cadence stretched first, hard stop until the minute turns.
9. Proxy and CA need only `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1`; nothing changes on the owner's machine.
10. Effort: step one half a day; REST source about five engineer-days (one specialist run plus two fix rounds), tests included, with the acceptance run in this container.

## PM amendments after Codex plan round 1 (2026-10-26)

This is a transport brief, not an indicator brief; the plan gate's first
criterion (a trading question) does not apply and is noted as such. The
scope is staged, and stage 3 is NOT part of this build:

**Stage A, build first:** the endpoint overrides `TT_BINANCE_SPOT_WS_BASE_URL`
and `TT_BINANCE_SPOT_REST_BASE_URL` exactly as section 0 specifies (config
registry, documentation rows, one URL test). Perpetual constants untouched.

**Stage B, this build:** `TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT=rest` serving SPOT candles and
trades only. The book is reported `status: "UNAVAILABLE"` with reason
`REST_NO_DEPTH`, so every depth indicator shows its own unavailable state and
the L2 engine is never fed sampled snapshots as if they were a stream.
Requirements Codex added, all accepted:
- Bars: a per-pair bar cursor by open time; after any outage longer than one
  minute, catch up by fetching klines from the last confirmed close forward
  (bounded pages) before resuming the two-row poll; classify EVERY row by its
  open time and close time against the exchange `serverTime`, never by row
  position or local clock; a revision replaces the same bar, finalisation
  happens once, and a response from an older poll never overwrites a newer
  revision.
- Trades: aggregate trades are labelled as such everywhere (`trade_id` from
  the aggregate id; health reason and documentation say "aggregate trades:
  fills from one taker order at one price are combined"). Parity against the
  WebSocket path is measured on summed sided volume over aligned complete
  minutes, never per print. A detected id gap marks the affected window
  incomplete (the tape must not read as LIVE_COMPLETE for it) until paging
  has caught up, and the server's aggressive-prints pool is only extended
  with complete ranges.
- Per-pair serialisation: one in-flight request per family per pair,
  cancellation on unsubscribe, responses from an obsolete subscription
  rejected.
- HTTP client: preserve status code and response headers for 429 and 418
  (Retry-After, x-mbx-used-weight-1m) instead of collapsing both to
  RATE_LIMITED; back off per Retry-After; 418 stops polling for the ban
  window.
- Weight: reserve the projected weight before dispatch (klines weight 2,
  aggTrades weight 4 per page, exchangeInfo probes, catch-up pages); default
  budget 4000 per minute, maximum setting 5000 so the 1000 margin always
  holds; if mandatory trade polling alone would exceed the budget, lengthen
  the trade cadence and say so in the health reason; never exceed the budget
  to keep a cadence.
- Health: the trader-facing reason is `Polled spot data · aggregate trades ·
  no order book`, with the degraded variants `rate limited`, `poll failed`,
  `catching up`; documentation names every unavailable analytic.
- Tests add: a multi-minute outage with catch-up, clock skew (machine ahead
  of exchange), a genuine multi-fill aggregate, a paging backlog, unsubscribe
  during an in-flight request, 429 then 418, budget reservation under
  concurrency.
- Acceptance: a connected-WebSocket run on a machine that has one is the
  semantic baseline (completed candles equal, aligned sided volume equal
  within rounding); the blocked-WebSocket run in this container is the
  availability test (chart, candles, trades, health reason visible).

**Stage C, deferred to its own brief:** depth from REST snapshots, only with
explicit coverage reconciliation in the L2 engine (`coverageOf` on every
snapshot, gap frames on every failed poll) and an honest statement that
replenishment and cancellation between snapshots are unobservable.

## PM amendments after Codex plan round 2 (2026-10-26)

Stage B is reduced to REST candles only (OHLC, volume, taker-buy volume,
trade count; forming and closed). Aggregate trades are deferred to Stage B2
because the web app's completeness check compares received trade records with
the candle's `trade_count` and the SDK trade contract promises individual
prints; a capability contract for aggregated tape is needed first. In this
build the tape and the book are both unavailable and the health reason reads
`Polled spot candles · no tape · no order book`. Added: `serverTime` fetch,
refresh and weight; catch-up page limits and continuation cursor with a
`catching up` reason; a forming-bar change detector over every downstream
field; verification that seeded L2 visuals turn unavailable; the semantic
baseline defined on overlapping complete minutes with OHLC, volume and
taker-buy volume equal within rounding; the weight guarantee stated as this
runtime's reserved dispatch budget. Earlier wording on per-print parity, READY
book and weight numbers is superseded. Three plan rounds used; the PM
approves the brief at this scope.

-function restBaseUrlFor(marketKind) {
+function isBareOrigin(url) {
+  return url.username === "" && url.password === "" && url.search === "" && url.hash === "" &&
+    (url.pathname === "/" || url.pathname === "");
+}
+
+function normalizeEndpointOrigin(name, raw) {
+  const rule = ENDPOINT_RULES[name];
+  let url = null;
+  if (typeof raw === "string") {
+    try {
+      url = new URL(raw.trim());
+    } catch {
+      url = null;
+    }
+  }
+  if (url === null || !rule.protocols.includes(url.protocol) || !isBareOrigin(url)) {
+    throw new Error(
+      `binance-config: ${rule.env} must be a bare ${rule.protocols.map((p) => p.slice(0, -1)).join(" or ")} origin without path, query or credentials`,
+    );
+  }
+  return url.origin;
+}
+
+/** The spot endpoints with any override applied and validated; USD-M has none. */
+function resolveBinanceEndpoints(overrides = {}) {
+  if (overrides === null || typeof overrides !== "object" || Array.isArray(overrides)) {
+    throw new Error("binance-config: endpoints must be a plain object");
+  }
+  const resolved = { ...DEFAULT_BINANCE_ENDPOINTS };
+  for (const key of Object.keys(overrides)) {
+    if (!Object.hasOwn(ENDPOINT_RULES, key)) {
+      throw new Error(`binance-config: unknown endpoint override "${key}"`);
+    }
+    if (overrides[key] === undefined) continue;
+    resolved[key] = normalizeEndpointOrigin(key, overrides[key]);
+  }
+  return Object.freeze(resolved);
+}
+
+/** Reads the spot endpoint overrides from the environment; an empty value counts as unset. */
+function binanceEndpointsFromEnv(env = process.env) {
+  const overrides = {};
+  const ws = env.TT_BINANCE_SPOT_WS_BASE_URL;
+  if (typeof ws === "string" && ws.trim() !== "") overrides.spotWsBaseUrl = ws;
+  const rest = env.TT_BINANCE_SPOT_REST_BASE_URL;
+  if (typeof rest === "string" && rest.trim() !== "") overrides.spotRestBaseUrl = rest;
+  return resolveBinanceEndpoints(overrides);
+}
+

-function delay(ms) {
-  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
+/** Resolves true after `ms`, or false at once when `signal` aborts first. */
+function delay(ms, signal) {
+  return new Promise((resolve) => {
+    if (signal?.aborted) {
+      resolve(false);
+      return;
+    }
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      signal?.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
+      resolve(true);
+    }, ms);
+    function onAbort() {
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      resolve(false);
+    }
+    signal?.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
+  });
 }
 
-function createBinanceKlineBackfill({ fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch, telemetry } = {}) {
+/**
+ * `now` is the clock candles are classified against and `finalizeGraceMs` the
+ * time after a candle's close before it counts as final: the stream transport
+ * uses the machine clock with no grace, the REST transport its synchronised
+ * exchange clock with the same grace as its live polling, so a candle the
+ * exchange still has open is never stored as closed history.
+ */
+function createBinanceKlineBackfill({
+  fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
+  fetchKlinesImpl = fetchKlines,
+  telemetry,
+  endpoints = DEFAULT_BINANCE_ENDPOINTS,
+  now = () => Date.now(),

+const { MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST } = require("./binance-http-client");
+
+const DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS = 1000;
+const TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT = 2;
+// A page that starts past the continuation cursor is asked for again this
+// many times before the missing minutes are given up as not supplied.
+const DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES = 3;
+
+/**
+ * The cursor over one pair's candles polled from /api/v3/klines. Every row is
+ * classified by its own open and close time against the exchange clock as it
+ * stood when the request was DISPATCHED, never by its position in the
+ * response, by the machine clock or by the time of receipt: the exchange may
+ * have built a row before the candle closed, so a row counts as final only
+ * when the request left after that candle's close plus a grace; otherwise it
+ * is the forming bar, re-sent as a revision whenever its values changed, and
+ * a later poll confirms it. A response from an older poll never overwrites a
+ * newer one. A page that starts beyond the next expected minute leaves the
+ * continuation cursor on the first missing minute while the exchange is asked
+ * again; after bounded retries the interval is recorded as missing and the
+ * cursor moves past it, so the gap is reported rather than silently skipped.
+ */
+function createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs, finalizeGraceMs = DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS, maxHoleRetries = DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES } = {}) {
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(intervalMs) || intervalMs <= 0) throw new Error("intervalMs must be a positive safe integer");
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(finalizeGraceMs) || finalizeGraceMs < 0) throw new Error("finalizeGraceMs must be a non-negative safe integer");
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maxHoleRetries) || maxHoleRetries <= 0) throw new Error("maxHoleRetries must be a positive safe integer");
+
+  let confirmedOpenTime = null;
+  let lastSeq = 0;
+  let holes = 0;
+  let holeRetries = 0;
+  let repairing = null; // { fromOpenTime, toOpenTime } while the exchange is asked again for missing minutes
+  const missing = [];
+  const forming = new Map();
+
+  function currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) {
+    return Math.floor(exchangeNowMs / intervalMs) * intervalMs;
+  }
+
+  /** After an outage the previous closed bar lies more than one bar past the last confirmed close. */
+  function needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) {
+    return confirmedOpenTime !== null && currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs > confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs;
+  }
+
+  function nextRequest(exchangeNowMs) {
+    if (needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null) {
+      return Object.freeze({ startTime: confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs, limit: MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST, catchUp: true });
+    }
+    return Object.freeze({ startTime: currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs, limit: TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT, catchUp: false });
+  }
+
+  function signatureOf(row) {
+    return `${row.open}|${row.high}|${row.low}|${row.close}|${row.volume}|${row.numberOfTrades}|${row.takerBuyBaseVolume}`;
+  }
+
+  function outcome(fields, exchangeNowMs) {
+    return Object.freeze({
+      stale: false,
+      emit: Object.freeze([]),
+      hole: null,
+      abandoned: null,
+      repairing: repairing !== null,
+      catchingUp: needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null,
+      ...fields,
+    });
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * `rows` are parsed kline rows ascending by open time (parseKlineRows);
+   * `exchangeNowMs` is the exchange time when their request was dispatched;
+   * `seq` orders the polls that produced them.
+   */
+  function apply({ rows, exchangeNowMs, seq }) {
+    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(seq) || seq <= lastSeq) {
+      return outcome({ stale: true }, exchangeNowMs);
+    }
+    lastSeq = seq;
+    const pending = rows.filter((row) => confirmedOpenTime === null || row.openTime > confirmedOpenTime);
+    let hole = null;
+    let abandoned = null;
+    if (pending.length > 0 && confirmedOpenTime !== null && pending[0].openTime > confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs) {
+      hole = Object.freeze({ fromOpenTime: confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs, toOpenTime: pending[0].openTime - intervalMs });
+      if (repairing === null || repairing.fromOpenTime !== hole.fromOpenTime) {
+        holes += 1;
+        holeRetries = 0;
+        repairing = hole;
+      }
+      holeRetries += 1;
+      if (holeRetries < maxHoleRetries) {
+        // Ask again from the first missing minute before believing the gap.
+        return outcome({ hole }, exchangeNowMs);
+      }
+      // The exchange did not supply the interval: record it as missing and
+      // move the cursor past it so the candles it does have are delivered.
+      abandoned = hole;
+      missing.push(hole);
+      repairing = null;
+      holeRetries = 0;
+      confirmedOpenTime = hole.toOpenTime;
+    } else if (pending.length > 0) {
+      repairing = null;
+      holeRetries = 0;
+    }
+    const emit = [];
+    for (const row of pending) {
+      const closed = row.closeTime + finalizeGraceMs <= exchangeNowMs;
+      if (closed) {
+        emit.push(Object.freeze({ row, closed: true }));
+        confirmedOpenTime = row.openTime;
+        forming.delete(row.openTime);
+        continue;
+      }
+      const signature = signatureOf(row);
+      if (forming.get(row.openTime) === signature) continue;
+      forming.set(row.openTime, signature);
+      emit.push(Object.freeze({ row, closed: false }));
+    }
+    for (const openTime of forming.keys()) {
+      if (confirmedOpenTime !== null && openTime <= confirmedOpenTime) forming.delete(openTime);
+    }
+    return outcome({ emit: Object.freeze(emit), hole, abandoned }, exchangeNowMs);
+  }
+
+  function reset() {
+    confirmedOpenTime = null;
+    repairing = null;
+    holeRetries = 0;
+    forming.clear();
+  }
+
+  /** Minutes the exchange never supplied, summed over the recorded missing intervals. */
+  function missingBars() {
+    return missing.reduce((sum, gap) => sum + (gap.toOpenTime - gap.fromOpenTime) / intervalMs + 1, 0);
+  }
+
+  return Object.freeze({
+    nextRequest,
+    needsCatchUp,
+    apply,
+    reset,
+    get confirmedOpenTime() { return confirmedOpenTime; },
+    get repairing() { return repairing; },
+    get missing() { return Object.freeze([...missing]); },
+    missingBars,
+    get stats() { return Object.freeze({ confirmedOpenTime, holes, holeRetries, lastSeq, missingBars: missingBars() }); },
+  });
+}
+
+module.exports = { createBinanceRestBarCursor, DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS, DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES, TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT };

+// The REST polling transport for Binance SPOT candles: the same surface as
+// binance-connection.js (what market-data-server.js duck-types), emitting the
+// same canonical KLINE envelopes through the same normalizer, for a host that
+// cannot open a WebSocket. It serves 1m candles only: open, high, low, close,
+// volume, taker-buy volume and trade count, forming and closed. There is no
+// tape and no order book; both are reported unavailable, never approximated.

+} = require("./binance-rest-weight-budget");
+const { createBinanceRestBarCursor, DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS } = require("./binance-rest-bar-cursor");
+
+const TRANSPORTS = Object.freeze({ WS: "ws", REST: "rest" });
+const DEFAULT_POLL_MS = 1000;
+const MIN_POLL_MS = 250;
+// A catch-up page holds up to 1000 candles (the klines limit); at most this
+// many pages are fetched back to back before the poller yields for a cadence.
+const DEFAULT_MAX_CATCH_UP_PAGES = 10;
+const DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS = 60_000;
+const DEFAULT_BAN_MS = 120_000;
+const CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES_BEFORE_DISCONNECT = 3;

+const BACKOFF_MAX_DELAY_MS = 30_000;

+  RATE_LIMITED: "rate limited",
+  POLL_FAILED: "poll failed",
+  CATCHING_UP: "catching up",
+  SLOW_POLLING: "slow polling (weight budget)",
+});
+/** Minutes the exchange never supplied stay on the reason for the rest of the session. */
+function historyIncompleteVariant(missingBars) {
+  return `history incomplete (${missingBars} min missing)`;

+const SERVED_EVENT_TYPES = new Set([EVENT_TYPES.KLINE]);
+const TRANSPORT_FAILURE_REASONS = new Set([

+const PERPETUAL_STATUS = Object.freeze({ public: "DISCONNECTED", market: "DISCONNECTED" });

+function binanceTransportFromEnv(env = process.env) {

+function pollerKey(descriptor) {

+function createBinanceRestConnection(options = {}) {
+  const {
+    telemetry,
+    fetchImpl = globalThis.fetch,
+    endpoints = DEFAULT_BINANCE_ENDPOINTS,
+    pollMs = DEFAULT_POLL_MS,
+    weightBudgetPerMinute = DEFAULT_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE,
+    now = () => Date.now(),
+    random = Math.random,
+    probeSymbol = listAllowedSymbols()[0],
+    requestTimeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS,
+    clockRefreshMs = DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS,
+    maxCatchUpPages = DEFAULT_MAX_CATCH_UP_PAGES,
+    finalizeGraceMs = DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS,
+    defaultBanMs = DEFAULT_BAN_MS,
+  } = options;
+
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(pollMs) || pollMs < MIN_POLL_MS) {
+    throw new Error(`pollMs must be a safe integer of at least ${MIN_POLL_MS}.`);
+  }
+  for (const [name, value] of [
+    ["requestTimeoutMs", requestTimeoutMs],
+    ["clockRefreshMs", clockRefreshMs],
+    ["maxCatchUpPages", maxCatchUpPages],
+    ["defaultBanMs", defaultBanMs],
+  ]) {
+    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value <= 0) throw new Error(`${name} must be a positive safe integer.`);
+  }
+  if (typeof now !== "function" || typeof random !== "function") {
+    throw new Error("now and random must be functions.");
+  }
+  const resolvedEndpoints = resolveBinanceEndpoints(endpoints);
+  const spotBaseUrl = restBaseUrlFor(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, resolvedEndpoints);
+  const marketKindContexts = Object.freeze({
+    SPOT: createBinanceMarketKindContext(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, resolvedEndpoints),
+    PERPETUAL: createBinanceMarketKindContext(MARKET_KINDS.PERPETUAL, resolvedEndpoints),
+  });
+  const feedHealth = createBinanceFeedHealthMonitor({ now });
+  const budget = createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: weightBudgetPerMinute, now });
+  // The backfill classifies candles against this transport's exchange clock
+  // with its grace, and every page goes through the shared dispatch gate.
+  const klineBackfill = createBinanceKlineBackfill({
+    fetchKlinesImpl: gatedFetchKlines,
+    telemetry,
+    endpoints: resolvedEndpoints,
+    now: exchangeNow,
+    finalizeGraceMs,
+  });
+
+  const messageListeners = new Set();
+  const errorListeners = new Set();
+  const pollers = new Map();
+  const probe = { inflight: null, timer: null, attempt: 0 };
+  const clock = { inflight: null, timer: null };
+  const gatedControllers = new Set();
+  let accepting = false;
+  let sessionGeneration = 0;
+  let routeState = "DISCONNECTED";
+  let exchangeOffsetMs = 0;
+  let clockSyncedAtMs = null;
+  let banUntilMs = 0;
+  let rateLimitedUntilMs = 0;
+  let consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
+  let plan = planCadence({ klinePollers: 0, pollMs, budgetPerMinute: weightBudgetPerMinute });
+  const counters = { polls: 0, pollFailures: 0, budgetDeferrals: 0, emitted: 0, probes: 0, clockSyncs: 0, clockRefreshFailures: 0 };
+
+  /** The exchange's clock as this process last measured it; rows are classified against it, never against the machine. */
+  function exchangeNow() {
+    return now() + exchangeOffsetMs;
+  }
+
+  function telemetryEvent(name, fields = {}) {
+    emitTelemetry(telemetry, `market_data.binance_rest.${name}`, fields);
+  }
+
+  function safelyInvokeListener(listener, payload) {
+    try {
+      listener(payload);
+    } catch (error) {
+      console.error("binance rest listener failed", error?.message ?? error);
+    }
+  }
+
+  function notifyMessageListeners(envelope) {
+    counters.emitted += 1;
+    for (const listener of [...messageListeners]) safelyInvokeListener(listener, envelope);
+  }
+
+  function notifyErrorListeners(payload) {
+    for (const listener of [...errorListeners]) safelyInvokeListener(listener, Object.freeze(payload));
+  }
+
+  /** Resolves true after `ms`, or false at once when `signal` aborts first. */
+  function sleep(ms, signal) {
+    return new Promise((resolve) => {
+      if (signal.aborted) {
+        resolve(false);
+        return;
+      }
+      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
+        signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
+        resolve(true);
+      }, Math.max(1, ms));
+      timer.unref?.();
+      function onAbort() {
+        clearTimeout(timer);
+        resolve(false);
+      }
+      signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
+    });
+  }
+
+  // The dispatch gate every request of this transport passes: it waits out a
+  // ban or rate-limit pause, then reserves the request's weight, waiting for
+  // the next minute when the budget is spent. False means the wait was aborted.
+  async function awaitDispatch(weight, signal) {
+    for (;;) {
+      if (signal.aborted) return false;
+      const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
+      if (wait > 0) {
+        if (!(await sleep(wait, signal))) return false;
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (budget.reserve(weight)) return true;
+      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
+      if (!(await sleep(budget.msUntilReset(), signal))) return false;
+    }
+  }
+
+  // A request issued on behalf of another module (the candle backfill, the
+  // tick-size lookup): gated, weighed, its headers read back, its failure fed
+  // to the shared rate-limit, ban and route handling, and abortable by the
+  // caller's signal or by disconnect().
+  async function gatedRequest(weight, externalSignal, perform) {
+    if (externalSignal !== undefined && !(externalSignal instanceof AbortSignal)) {
+      throw new Error("signal must be an AbortSignal when provided");
+    }
+    const controller = new AbortController();
+    const forwardAbort = () => controller.abort();
+    if (externalSignal?.aborted) controller.abort();
+    else externalSignal?.addEventListener("abort", forwardAbort, { once: true });
+    gatedControllers.add(controller);
+    try {
+      if (!(await awaitDispatch(weight, controller.signal))) return Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "ABORTED" });
+      let result;
+      try {
+        result = await perform(controller.signal);
+      } catch (error) {
+        result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: error?.name === "AbortError" ? "ABORTED" : "NETWORK_ERROR" });
+      }
+      observeWeight(result);
+      if (!result.ok && result.reason !== "ABORTED") noteRequestFailure(result);
+      return result;
+    } finally {
+      gatedControllers.delete(controller);
+      externalSignal?.removeEventListener("abort", forwardAbort);
+    }
+  }
+
+  function gatedFetchKlines(marketKind, symbol, options = {}) {
+    return gatedRequest(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES, options.signal, (signal) =>
+      fetchKlines(marketKind, symbol, { ...options, fetchImpl, timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs, signal }));
+  }
+
+  /** exchangeInfo for another module (the tick-size resolver), charged 20 through the same gate. */
+  function fetchExchangeInfoGated(marketKind, symbol, options = {}) {
+    return gatedRequest(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.EXCHANGE_INFO, options.signal, (signal) =>
+      fetchExchangeInfo(marketKind, symbol, {
+        ...options,
+        fetchImpl,
+        baseUrl: restBaseUrlFor(marketKind, resolvedEndpoints),
+        timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs,
+        signal,
+      }));
+  }
+
+  function setRouteState(next, fields = {}) {
+    if (routeState === next) return;
+    routeState = next;
+    if (next === "CONNECTED") feedHealth.recordRouteConnected(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, ROUTE_NAME);
+    else feedHealth.recordRouteDisconnected(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, ROUTE_NAME);
+    telemetryEvent(next === "CONNECTED" ? "route_connected" : "route_disconnected", { state: next, ...fields });
+  }
+
+  function backoffDelayMs(attempt) {
+    const raw = Math.min(BACKOFF_BASE_DELAY_MS * 2 ** Math.max(0, attempt - 1), BACKOFF_MAX_DELAY_MS);
+    const jitter = raw * BACKOFF_JITTER_RATIO * (random() * 2 - 1);
+    return Math.max(0, Math.round(raw + jitter));
+  }
+
+  function clearTimer(holder) {
+    if (holder.timer) clearTimeout(holder.timer);
+    holder.timer = null;
+  }
+
+  function schedule(holder, delayMs, run) {
+    clearTimer(holder);
+    const generationAtSchedule = sessionGeneration;
+    holder.timer = setTimeout(() => {
+      holder.timer = null;
+      if (!accepting || generationAtSchedule !== sessionGeneration) return;
+      run().catch((error) => {
+        notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: error?.message ?? String(error), code: "REST_INTERNAL" });
+      });
+    }, Math.max(0, delayMs));
+    holder.timer.unref?.();
+  }
+
+  function waitBeforeDispatchMs() {
+    const at = now();
+    return Math.max(0, banUntilMs - at, rateLimitedUntilMs - at);
+  }
+
+  function requestOptions(signal) {
+    return { fetchImpl, baseUrl: spotBaseUrl, timeoutMs: requestTimeoutMs, signal };
+  }
+
+  function observeWeight(result) {
+    const headers = result.ok ? result.value.headers : result.headers;
+    if (headers) budget.observe(headers);
+  }
+
+  function applyServerTime(serverTime, sentAt, receivedAt) {
+    exchangeOffsetMs = serverTime - Math.round((sentAt + receivedAt) / 2);
+    clockSyncedAtMs = receivedAt;
+    counters.clockSyncs += 1;
+  }
+
+  // The route is up once exchangeInfo (weight 20) answers: it proves the
+  // origin, the proxy and the symbol, and its serverTime seeds the exchange
+  // clock every candle is classified against.
+  async function runProbe() {
+    if (!accepting || probe.inflight) return;
+    const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
+    if (wait > 0) {
+      schedule(probe, wait, runProbe);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (!budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.EXCHANGE_INFO)) {
+      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
+      schedule(probe, budget.msUntilReset(), runProbe);
+      return;
+    }
+    const controller = new AbortController();
+    probe.inflight = controller;
+    const generation = sessionGeneration;
+    const sentAt = now();
+    counters.probes += 1;
+    let result;
+    try {
+      result = await fetchExchangeInfo(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, probeSymbol, requestOptions(controller.signal));
+    } catch {
+      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "NETWORK_ERROR" });
+    }
+    probe.inflight = null;
+    if (!accepting || generation !== sessionGeneration) return;
+    const receivedAt = now();
+    observeWeight(result);
+    const serverTime = result.ok ? result.value.body?.serverTime : undefined;
+    if (!result.ok || !Number.isSafeInteger(serverTime)) {
+      probe.attempt += 1;
+      telemetryEvent("probe_failed", { reason: result.ok ? "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" : result.reason, attempt: probe.attempt });
+      if (!result.ok) noteRequestFailure(result);
+      schedule(probe, Math.max(waitBeforeDispatchMs(), backoffDelayMs(probe.attempt)), runProbe);
+      return;
+    }
+    probe.attempt = 0;
+    applyServerTime(serverTime, sentAt, receivedAt);
+    consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
+    setRouteState("CONNECTED");
+    scheduleClockRefresh();
+    for (const poller of pollers.values()) schedulePoll(poller, 0);
+  }
+
+  // The exchange clock is re-read from /api/v3/time (weight 1, reserved like
+  // every request) once a minute; a failed refresh keeps the last offset.
+  function scheduleClockRefresh() {
+    schedule(clock, clockRefreshMs, refreshClock);
+  }
+
+  async function refreshClock() {
+    if (!accepting || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || clock.inflight) return;
+    if (waitBeforeDispatchMs() > 0 || !budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.SERVER_TIME)) {
+      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
+      scheduleClockRefresh();
+      return;
+    }
+    const controller = new AbortController();
+    clock.inflight = controller;
+    const generation = sessionGeneration;
+    const sentAt = now();
+    let result;
+    try {
+      result = await fetchServerTime(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, requestOptions(controller.signal));
+    } catch {
+      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "NETWORK_ERROR" });
+    }
+    clock.inflight = null;
+    if (!accepting || generation !== sessionGeneration) return;
+    observeWeight(result);
+    if (result.ok && Number.isSafeInteger(result.value.body?.serverTime)) {
+      applyServerTime(result.value.body.serverTime, sentAt, now());
+    } else {
+      // The last valid offset stays; the failure itself gets the shared
+      // handling, so a 429 pauses polling and a 418 opens the ban window.
+      counters.clockRefreshFailures += 1;
+      telemetryEvent("clock_refresh_failed", { reason: result.ok ? "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" : result.reason, status: result.status ?? null });
+      if (!result.ok && result.reason !== "ABORTED") noteRequestFailure(result);
+    }
+    scheduleClockRefresh();
+  }
+
+  // Bookkeeping shared by every failed request: 429 pauses everything until
+  // Retry-After (or the backoff); 418 is an IP ban that stops polling for the
+  // whole window; three transport failures in a row mean the route is down.
+  function noteRequestFailure(result) {
+    counters.pollFailures += 1;
+    const at = now();
+    if (result.reason === "IP_BANNED") {
+      banUntilMs = Math.max(banUntilMs, at + (result.retryAfterMs ?? defaultBanMs));
+      telemetryEvent("ip_banned", { untilMs: banUntilMs, retryAfterMs: result.retryAfterMs ?? null });
+      notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: "Binance banned this IP; polling stops for the Retry-After window.", code: 418 });
+      setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: "IP_BANNED" });
+      schedule(probe, banUntilMs - at, runProbe);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (result.reason === "RATE_LIMITED") {
+      rateLimitedUntilMs = Math.max(rateLimitedUntilMs, at + (result.retryAfterMs ?? backoffDelayMs(1)));
+      telemetryEvent("rate_limited", { untilMs: rateLimitedUntilMs, retryAfterMs: result.retryAfterMs ?? null });
+      return;
+    }
+    if (TRANSPORT_FAILURE_REASONS.has(result.reason)) {
+      consecutiveTransportFailures += 1;
+      if (routeState === "CONNECTED" && consecutiveTransportFailures >= CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES_BEFORE_DISCONNECT) {
+        notifyErrorListeners({ marketKind: MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, route: ROUTE_NAME, message: "Binance REST polling failed repeatedly; the route is down until a probe succeeds." });
+        setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: result.reason });
+        schedule(probe, backoffDelayMs(1), runProbe);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  function replan() {
+    plan = planCadence({ klinePollers: pollers.size, pollMs, budgetPerMinute: weightBudgetPerMinute });
+  }
+
+  function createPoller(descriptor) {
+    return {
+      descriptor,
+      key: pollerKey(descriptor),
+      timer: null,
+      inflight: null,
+      epoch: 0,
+      seq: 0,
+      attempt: 0,
+      catchUpPages: 0,
+      catchingUp: false,
+      repairing: false,
+      lastFailureReason: null,
+      lastLatencyMs: null,
+      lastSuccessAtMs: null,
+      lastSentAtMs: null,
+      budgetDeferred: false,
+      bars: createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: candleIntervalMsFor(descriptor.interval), finalizeGraceMs }),
+    };
+  }
+
+  function resetPollerState(poller) {
+    poller.bars.reset();
+    poller.catchUpPages = 0;
+    poller.catchingUp = false;
+    poller.repairing = false;
+    poller.attempt = 0;
+  }
+
+  function schedulePoll(poller, delayMs) {
+    schedule(poller, delayMs, () => runPoll(poller));
+  }
+
+  function scheduleNextPoll(poller, { immediate = false } = {}) {
+    if (immediate) {
+      schedulePoll(poller, 0);
+      return;
+    }
+    const elapsed = poller.lastSentAtMs === null ? 0 : now() - poller.lastSentAtMs;
+    schedulePoll(poller, Math.max(0, plan.klineCadenceMs - elapsed));
+  }
+
+  function beginRecovery(poller) {
+    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
+    feedHealth.beginRecovery(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval);
+  }
+
+  function endRecovery(poller) {
+    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
+    feedHealth.endRecovery(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval);
+  }
+
+  async function runPoll(poller) {
+    if (!accepting || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || poller.inflight || pollers.get(poller.key) !== poller) return;
+    const wait = waitBeforeDispatchMs();
+    if (wait > 0) {
+      schedulePoll(poller, wait);
+      return;
+    }
+    // Weight is reserved before dispatch; a refusal waits for the next minute.
+    if (!budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES)) {
+      counters.budgetDeferrals += 1;
+      poller.budgetDeferred = true;
+      telemetryEvent("poll_deferred_by_budget", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(poller.descriptor.symbol) });
+      schedulePoll(poller, budget.msUntilReset());
+      return;
+    }
+    poller.budgetDeferred = false;
+    const controller = new AbortController();
+    const epochAtRequest = poller.epoch;
+    const generationAtRequest = sessionGeneration;
+    const seq = ++poller.seq;
+    poller.inflight = controller;
+    poller.lastSentAtMs = now();
+    counters.polls += 1;
+    // Finality is judged by the exchange clock as it stood when the request
+    // left: the exchange may build a row before the candle closes, so a row
+    // received after the close is final only if it was asked for after it.
+    const dispatchedAtExchangeMs = exchangeNow();
+    const request = poller.bars.nextRequest(dispatchedAtExchangeMs);
+    let result;
+    try {
+      result = await fetchKlines(MARKET_KINDS.SPOT, poller.descriptor.symbol, {
+        ...requestOptions(controller.signal),
+        interval: poller.descriptor.interval,
+        startTime: request.startTime,
+        limit: request.limit,
+      });
+    } catch (error) {
+      result = Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: error?.name === "AbortError" ? "ABORTED" : "NETWORK_ERROR" });
+    }
+    if (poller.inflight === controller) poller.inflight = null;
+    // A response for a subscription that ended, or from a session that was
+    // disconnected, is thrown away whatever it says.
+    if (!accepting || generationAtRequest !== sessionGeneration || epochAtRequest !== poller.epoch || pollers.get(poller.key) !== poller) return;
+    const receivedAt = now();
+    poller.lastLatencyMs = receivedAt - poller.lastSentAtMs;
+    observeWeight(result);
+    if (!result.ok) {
+      onPollFailure(poller, result);
+      return;
+    }
+    // The conservative estimate: the pre-dispatch reading, or lower if the
+    // clock was re-synchronised meanwhile and the exchange turned out behind.
+    const finalityNowMs = Math.min(dispatchedAtExchangeMs, poller.lastSentAtMs + exchangeOffsetMs);
+    const outcome = applyKlinePage(poller, result.value.body, receivedAt, seq, finalityNowMs);
+    if (outcome.malformed) {
+      onPollFailure(poller, Object.freeze({ ok: false, reason: "MALFORMED_RESPONSE_BODY" }));
+      return;
+    }
+    consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
+    poller.attempt = 0;
+    poller.lastFailureReason = null;
+    poller.lastSuccessAtMs = receivedAt;
+    if (outcome.complete) endRecovery(poller);
+    scheduleNextPoll(poller, { immediate: outcome.immediate });
+  }
+
+  function onPollFailure(poller, result) {
+    poller.attempt += 1;
+    poller.lastFailureReason = result.reason;
+    poller.catchUpPages = 0;
+    beginRecovery(poller);
+    telemetryEvent("poll_failed", {
+      symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(poller.descriptor.symbol),
+      reason: result.reason,
+      status: result.status ?? null,
+      attempt: poller.attempt,
+    });
+    noteRequestFailure(result);
+    if (routeState !== "CONNECTED") return;
+    schedulePoll(poller, Math.max(waitBeforeDispatchMs(), backoffDelayMs(poller.attempt)));
+  }
+
+  function emitEnvelope(poller, raw, receivedAtMs) {
+    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
+    const envelope = normalizeBinanceEvent({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, raw }, { receivedAtMs });
+    const health = feedHealth.computeContext({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, routeConnected: routeState === "CONNECTED", nowMs: now() });
+    notifyMessageListeners(Object.freeze({ ...envelope, stale: health.stale, recoveryState: health.recoveryState }));
+  }
+
+  function applyKlinePage(poller, body, receivedAt, seq, finalityNowMs) {
+    const { marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval } = poller.descriptor;
+    const rows = parseKlineRows(body, candleIntervalMsFor(interval));
+    if (rows === null) return { malformed: true };
+    // A successful poll is an observation of the pair; only a failed poll
+    // leaves the feed to go stale, as a dead stream would.
+    feedHealth.recordEvent(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, receivedAt);
+    const outcome = poller.bars.apply({ rows, exchangeNowMs: finalityNowMs, seq });
+    if (outcome.stale) {
+      telemetryEvent("kline_poll_superseded", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol) });
+      return { malformed: false, complete: !poller.catchingUp && !poller.repairing, immediate: false };
+    }
+    if (outcome.abandoned !== null) {
+      // Bounded retries did not bring the minutes: they are reported missing
+      // from here on, and the candles the exchange does have are delivered.
+      poller.repairing = false;
+      telemetryEvent("kline_hole_abandoned", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol), ...outcome.abandoned, missingBars: poller.bars.missingBars() });
+    } else if (outcome.hole !== null) {
+      // The page started past the next expected minute: stay degraded, keep
+      // the cursor on the first missing minute and ask again at the cadence.
+      if (!poller.repairing) telemetryEvent("kline_hole_detected", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol), ...outcome.hole });
+      poller.repairing = true;
+      beginRecovery(poller);
+      return { malformed: false, complete: false, immediate: false };
+    }
+    poller.repairing = false;
+    for (const { row, closed } of outcome.emit) {
+      emitEnvelope(poller, {
+        e: "kline",
+        E: receivedAt,
+        s: symbol,
+        k: {
+          t: row.openTime,
+          T: row.closeTime,
+          s: symbol,
+          i: interval,
+          o: row.open,
+          c: row.close,
+          h: row.high,
+          l: row.low,
+          v: row.volume,
+          n: row.numberOfTrades,
+          x: closed,
+          q: row.quoteVolume,
+          V: row.takerBuyBaseVolume,
+          Q: row.takerBuyQuoteVolume,
+        },
+      }, receivedAt);
+    }
+    if (outcome.catchingUp) {
+      // Behind by more than one bar: the next request continues from the
+      // confirmed close, in pages of up to 1000, at most maxCatchUpPages in a
+      // row before the poller yields for a cadence and continues.
+      if (!poller.catchingUp) telemetryEvent("kline_catch_up_started", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol), confirmedOpenTime: poller.bars.confirmedOpenTime });
+      poller.catchingUp = true;
+      beginRecovery(poller);
+      poller.catchUpPages += 1;
+      if (poller.catchUpPages >= maxCatchUpPages) {
+        poller.catchUpPages = 0;
+        return { malformed: false, complete: false, immediate: false };
+      }
+      return { malformed: false, complete: false, immediate: true };
+    }
+    if (poller.catchingUp) telemetryEvent("kline_catch_up_completed", { symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(symbol), confirmedOpenTime: poller.bars.confirmedOpenTime });
+    poller.catchingUp = false;
+    poller.catchUpPages = 0;
+    return { malformed: false, complete: true, immediate: false };
+  }
+
+  function connect() {
+    accepting = true;
+    for (const poller of pollers.values()) resetPollerState(poller);
+    if (routeState === "DISCONNECTED") routeState = "CONNECTING";
+    schedule(probe, 0, runProbe);
+  }
+
+  function abortAll() {
+    for (const holder of [probe, clock, ...pollers.values()]) {
+      clearTimer(holder);
+      holder.inflight?.abort();
+      holder.inflight = null;
+    }
+    for (const controller of gatedControllers) controller.abort();
+  }
+
+  function disconnect() {
+    accepting = false;
+    sessionGeneration += 1;
+    abortAll();
+    probe.attempt = 0;
+    banUntilMs = 0;
+    rateLimitedUntilMs = 0;
+    consecutiveTransportFailures = 0;
+    for (const poller of pollers.values()) resetPollerState(poller);
+    setRouteState("DISCONNECTED", { reason: "DISCONNECT" });
+  }
+
+  function getStatus() {
+    return Object.freeze({
+      SPOT: Object.freeze({ public: routeState }),
+      PERPETUAL: PERPETUAL_STATUS,
+    });
+  }
+
+  function registryFor(marketKind) {
+    return marketKindContexts[marketKind].subscriptionRegistry;
+  }
+
+  function subscribeMany(requests, consumerId) {
+    assertConsumerId(consumerId);
+    if (!Array.isArray(requests) || requests.length === 0) {
+      throw new Error("subscribeMany() requires a non-empty array of subscription requests.");
+    }
+    const descriptors = requests.map((request) => describeBinanceSubscription(request));
+    const added = [];
+    const rollback = () => {
+      for (const descriptor of added) {
+        registryFor(descriptor.marketKind).remove(descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, consumerId, descriptor.interval);
+      }
+    };
+    for (const descriptor of descriptors) {
+      const isNewOnWire = registryFor(descriptor.marketKind).add(descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, consumerId, undefined, descriptor.interval);
+      if (isNewOnWire) added.push(descriptor);
+    }
+    for (const descriptor of descriptors) {
+      // Perpetuals have no REST mirror and are not served by this transport;
+      // their route never connects, exactly as the stream transport reports a
+      // route that is not up.
+      if (descriptor.marketKind === MARKET_KINDS.PERPETUAL || routeState !== "CONNECTED") {
+        rollback();
+        throw new Error(`Cannot subscribe before the Binance ${descriptor.marketKind} ${ROUTE_NAME} route is connected.`);
+      }
+    }
+    for (const descriptor of added) {
+      // Trades, book ticker and depth are accepted so the server's bookkeeping
+      // is unchanged, but nothing is polled for them: they are unavailable.
+      if (!SERVED_EVENT_TYPES.has(descriptor.eventType)) continue;
+      const poller = createPoller(descriptor);
+      pollers.set(poller.key, poller);
+      beginRecovery(poller);
+      schedulePoll(poller, 0);
+    }
+    replan();
+    return added.length;
+  }
+
+  function subscribe(request, consumerId) {
+    return subscribeMany([request], consumerId) === 1;
+  }
+
+  function teardownDescriptor(descriptor) {
+    const key = pollerKey(descriptor);
+    const poller = pollers.get(key);
+    if (!poller) return;
+    pollers.delete(key);
+    poller.epoch += 1;
+    clearTimer(poller);
+    poller.inflight?.abort();
+    poller.inflight = null;
+    feedHealth.endRecovery(descriptor.marketKind, descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, descriptor.interval);
+  }
+
+  function unsubscribeMany(requests, consumerId) {
+    assertConsumerId(consumerId);
+    if (!Array.isArray(requests) || requests.length === 0) {
+      throw new Error("unsubscribeMany() requires a non-empty array of subscription requests.");
+    }
+    const descriptors = requests.map((request) => describeBinanceSubscription(request));
+    let released = 0;
+    for (const descriptor of descriptors) {
+      const lastHolderReleased = registryFor(descriptor.marketKind).remove(descriptor.symbol, descriptor.eventType, consumerId, descriptor.interval);
+      if (!lastHolderReleased) continue;
+      released += 1;
+      teardownDescriptor(descriptor);
+    }
+    replan();
+    return released;
+  }
+
+  function unsubscribe(request, consumerId) {
+    return unsubscribeMany([request], consumerId) === 1;
+  }
+
+  function removeConsumer(consumerId) {
+    assertConsumerId(consumerId);
+    const released = [];
+    for (const marketKind of Object.values(MARKET_KINDS)) {
+      for (const tuple of registryFor(marketKind).removeConsumer(consumerId)) {
+        const descriptor = Object.freeze({
+          symbol: tuple.symbol,
+          marketKind,
+          eventType: tuple.eventType,
+          ...(tuple.interval !== undefined ? { interval: tuple.interval } : {}),
+        });
+        teardownDescriptor(descriptor);
+        released.push(descriptor);
+      }
+    }
+    replan();
+    return released;
+  }
+
+  function isSubscribed(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval) {
+    const context = marketKindContexts[marketKind];
+    if (!context) return false;
+    return context.subscriptionRegistry.list().some(
+      (entry) => entry.symbol === symbol && entry.eventType === eventType && (entry.interval ?? null) === (interval ?? null),
+    );
+  }
+
+  function recoverStaleSubscription(request, { automatic = false } = {}) {
+    if (typeof automatic !== "boolean") {
+      throw new Error("recoverStaleSubscription(): automatic must be a boolean when provided.");
+    }
+    const descriptor = describeBinanceSubscription(request);
+    const poller = pollers.get(pollerKey(descriptor));
+    if (!poller || routeState !== "CONNECTED" || poller.inflight || waitBeforeDispatchMs() > 0) return false;
+    telemetryEvent("stale_subscription_recovery_started", { eventType: descriptor.eventType, symbol: safeTelemetrySymbol(descriptor.symbol) });
+    beginRecovery(poller);
+    poller.attempt = 0;
+    schedulePoll(poller, 0);
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  function healthVariants(poller) {
+    const variants = [];
+    const at = now();
+    if (at < banUntilMs || at < rateLimitedUntilMs) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.RATE_LIMITED);
+    const pollFailed = poller !== null && poller.lastFailureReason !== null &&
+      poller.lastFailureReason !== "RATE_LIMITED" && poller.lastFailureReason !== "IP_BANNED";
+    if (routeState !== "CONNECTED" || pollFailed) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.POLL_FAILED);
+    if (poller !== null && (poller.catchingUp || poller.repairing)) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.CATCHING_UP);
+    if (plan.stretched || poller?.budgetDeferred) variants.push(HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS.SLOW_POLLING);
+    const missingBars = poller?.bars.missingBars() ?? 0;
+    if (missingBars > 0) variants.push(historyIncompleteVariant(missingBars));
+    return variants;
+  }
+
+  function healthReason(poller) {
+    return [HEALTH_REASON_BASE, ...healthVariants(poller)].join(" · ");
+  }
+
+  function getFeedHealth(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval) {
+    if (!isSubscribed(marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval)) return undefined;
+    if (marketKind !== MARKET_KINDS.SPOT || !SERVED_EVENT_TYPES.has(eventType)) {
+      // Not served at all: unavailable, and flagged so the server's worst-of
+      // health follows the candle feed while the reason names what is missing.
+      return Object.freeze({ stale: true, recoveryState: "UNAVAILABLE", transport: TRANSPORTS.REST, served: false, reason: healthReason(null) });
+    }
+    const poller = pollers.get(pollerKey({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval })) ?? null;
+    const context = feedHealth.computeContext({ marketKind, symbol, eventType, interval, routeConnected: routeState === "CONNECTED" });
+    return Object.freeze({
+      ...context,
+      transport: TRANSPORTS.REST,
+      served: true,
+      reason: healthReason(poller),
+      pollMs: plan.klineCadenceMs,
+      lastPollLatencyMs: poller?.lastLatencyMs ?? null,
+    });
+  }
+
+  function getOrderBook(marketKind, symbol) {
+    return isSubscribed(marketKind, symbol, EVENT_TYPES.DEPTH) ? BOOK_UNAVAILABLE : undefined;
+  }
+
+  function getDepthSnapshot() {
+    return undefined;
+  }
+
+  function onMessage(listener) {
+    if (typeof listener !== "function") throw new Error("Binance message listener must be a function.");
+    if (!messageListeners.has(listener) && messageListeners.size >= MAX_LISTENERS) {
+      throw new Error(`Cannot register more than ${MAX_LISTENERS} Binance message listeners.`);
+    }
+    messageListeners.add(listener);
+    return () => messageListeners.delete(listener);
+  }
+
+  function onError(listener) {
+    if (typeof listener !== "function") throw new Error("Binance error listener must be a function.");
+    if (!errorListeners.has(listener) && errorListeners.size >= MAX_LISTENERS) {
+      throw new Error(`Cannot register more than ${MAX_LISTENERS} Binance error listeners.`);
+    }
+    errorListeners.add(listener);
+    return () => errorListeners.delete(listener);
+  }
+
+  function getOperationalStats() {
+    let inflight = 0;
+    const pollerStats = [];
+    for (const poller of pollers.values()) {
+      if (poller.inflight) inflight += 1;
+      pollerStats.push(Object.freeze({
+        symbol: poller.descriptor.symbol,
+        interval: poller.descriptor.interval,
+        cadenceMs: plan.klineCadenceMs,
+        lastLatencyMs: poller.lastLatencyMs,
+        lastSuccessAtMs: poller.lastSuccessAtMs,
+        lastFailureReason: poller.lastFailureReason,
+        catchingUp: poller.catchingUp,
+        repairing: poller.repairing,
+        missingBars: poller.bars.missingBars(),
+        confirmedOpenTime: poller.bars.confirmedOpenTime,
+      }));
+    }
+    return Object.freeze({
+      transport: TRANSPORTS.REST,
+      routeState,
+      pollMs,
+      klineCadenceMs: plan.klineCadenceMs,
+      cadenceStretched: plan.stretched,
+      projectedWeightPerMinute: plan.projectedPerMinute,
+      weight: budget.stats(),
+      ...counters,
+      inflight: inflight + (probe.inflight ? 1 : 0) + (clock.inflight ? 1 : 0) + gatedControllers.size,
+      gatedRequests: gatedControllers.size,
+      exchangeOffsetMs,
+      clockSyncedAtMs,
+      rateLimitedUntilMs,
+      banUntilMs,
+      pollers: Object.freeze(pollerStats),
+    });
+  }
+
+  function quiesce({ timeoutMs = 30_000 } = {}) {
+    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(timeoutMs) || timeoutMs <= 0) {
+      return Promise.reject(new Error("quiesce timeoutMs must be a positive safe integer."));
+    }
+    accepting = false;
+    for (const holder of [probe, clock, ...pollers.values()]) clearTimer(holder);
+    // A backfill or lookup still queued at the gate would wait for a minute
+    // boundary or a ban to end; shutdown does not.
+    for (const controller of gatedControllers) controller.abort();
+    const deadline = now() + timeoutMs;
+    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+      const inspect = () => {
+        const { inflight } = getOperationalStats();
+        if (inflight === 0) {
+          resolve();
+          return;
+        }
+        if (now() >= deadline) {
+          reject(new Error(`Binance REST connection still has ${inflight} request(s) in flight at shutdown.`));
+          return;
+        }
+        setTimeout(inspect, 10);
+      };
+      inspect();
+    });
+  }
+
+  function fetchRecentKlines(marketKind, symbol, klineOptions) {
+    return klineBackfill.fetchRecentBars(marketKind, symbol, klineOptions);
+  }
+
+  return Object.freeze({
+    connect,
+    disconnect,
+    getStatus,
+    subscribe,
+    subscribeMany,
+    unsubscribe,
+    unsubscribeMany,
+    recoverStaleSubscription,
+    removeConsumer,
+    onMessage,
+    onError,
+    getDepthSnapshot,
+    getOrderBook,
+    getOrderBookStatus: getOrderBook,
+    getFeedHealth,
+    getOperationalStats,
+    quiesce,
+    fetchRecentKlines,
+    fetchExchangeInfo: fetchExchangeInfoGated,
+  });
+}
+
+module.exports = {
+  createBinanceRestConnection,
+  binanceTransportFromEnv,
+  TRANSPORTS,
+  DEFAULT_POLL_MS,
+  MIN_POLL_MS,
+  BOOK_UNAVAILABLE_REASON,
+  HEALTH_REASON_BASE,
+  HEALTH_REASON_VARIANTS,
+  historyIncompleteVariant,
+  DEFAULT_MAX_CATCH_UP_PAGES,
+  DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS,
+  CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES_BEFORE_DISCONNECT,
+};
diff --git a/apps/runtime/src/providers/binance/binance-rest-weight-budget.js b/apps/runtime/src/providers/binance/binance-rest-weight-budget.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..10c53fe
--- /dev/null
+++ b/apps/runtime/src/providers/binance/binance-rest-weight-budget.js
@@ -0,0 +1,154 @@
+// Binance's spot REST limit is REQUEST_WEIGHT 6000 per minute per IP, counted
+// by the exchange and reported back in every response's x-mbx-used-weight-1m
+// header. The REST transport reserves each request's weight here before it is
+// sent, against a budget that leaves at least 1000 of margin, and raises its
+// own count to whatever the exchange reports, so it never has to be told off.
+const REQUEST_WEIGHTS = Object.freeze({
+  KLINES: 2,
+  EXCHANGE_INFO: 20,
+  SERVER_TIME: 1,
+});
+const EXCHANGE_WEIGHT_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE = 6000;
+const DEFAULT_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE = 4000;
+const MAX_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE = 5000;
+const MIN_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE = 100;
+const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
+const USED_WEIGHT_HEADER = "x-mbx-used-weight-1m";
+// The share of the budget the steady cadence may plan on; the rest is kept
+// for the connect probe, the clock refresh, catch-up pages and the candle
+// backfill.
+const PLANNED_SHARE = 0.9;
+const MAX_CADENCE_STRETCH = 60;
+
+function assertBudget(budgetPerMinute) {
+  if (
+    !Number.isSafeInteger(budgetPerMinute) ||
+    budgetPerMinute < MIN_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE ||
+    budgetPerMinute > MAX_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE
+  ) {
+    throw new Error(
+      `weight budget must be a whole number between ${MIN_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE} and ${MAX_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE} per minute`,
+    );
+  }
+}
+
+function usedWeightFromHeaders(headers) {
+  if (headers === null || typeof headers !== "object") return null;
+  const entry = Object.entries(headers).find(([key]) => key.toLowerCase() === USED_WEIGHT_HEADER);
+  if (!entry || typeof entry[1] !== "string" || !/^\d+$/.test(entry[1].trim())) return null;
+  const value = Number.parseInt(entry[1].trim(), 10);
+  return Number.isSafeInteger(value) ? value : null;
+}
+
+function createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute = DEFAULT_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE, now = () => Date.now() } = {}) {
+  assertBudget(budgetPerMinute);
+  if (typeof now !== "function") throw new Error("now must be a function");
+
+  let minuteStart = Math.floor(now() / MINUTE_MS) * MINUTE_MS;
+  let reserved = 0;
+  let observed = 0;
+  // The conservative total this minute: every reservation adds to it, and an
+  // exchange header raises it when the exchange counts more than we did.
+  // Reservations after a header keep adding on top of that header.
+  let accounted = 0;
+  let deferrals = 0;
+  let reservations = 0;
+
+  function rollMinute() {
+    const current = Math.floor(now() / MINUTE_MS) * MINUTE_MS;
+    if (current !== minuteStart) {
+      minuteStart = current;
+      reserved = 0;
+      observed = 0;
+      accounted = 0;
+    }
+  }
+
+  function used() {
+    rollMinute();
+    return accounted;
+  }
+
+  /** Reserves `weight` for a request about to be sent; false means wait for the next minute. */
+  function reserve(weight) {
+    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(weight) || weight <= 0) throw new Error("weight must be a positive safe integer");
+    rollMinute();
+    if (accounted + weight > budgetPerMinute) {
+      deferrals += 1;
+      return false;
+    }
+    reserved += weight;
+    accounted += weight;
+    reservations += 1;
+    return true;
+  }
+
+  /** Raises this minute's count to what the exchange reports in a response's headers. */
+  function observe(headers) {
+    const value = usedWeightFromHeaders(headers);
+    if (value === null) return null;
+    rollMinute();
+    if (value > observed) observed = value;
+    if (value > accounted) accounted = value;
+    return value;
+  }
+
+  function msUntilReset() {
+    const at = now();
+    return Math.max(1, minuteStart + MINUTE_MS - at);
+  }
+
+  function stats() {
+    rollMinute();
+    return Object.freeze({
+      budgetPerMinute,
+      minuteStart,
+      reserved,
+      observed,
+      used: accounted,
+      remaining: Math.max(0, budgetPerMinute - accounted),
+      reservations,
+      deferrals,
+    });
+  }
+
+  return Object.freeze({ reserve, observe, used, msUntilReset, stats, get budgetPerMinute() { return budgetPerMinute; } });
+}
+
+/**
+ * The steady candle cadence that fits the budget: the poll interval, lengthened
+ * in whole multiples until the planned share holds. The caller says so in the
+ * health reason when it was lengthened. The budget is never exceeded to keep a
+ * cadence.
+ */
+function planCadence({ klinePollers, pollMs, budgetPerMinute }) {
+  assertBudget(budgetPerMinute);
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(pollMs) || pollMs <= 0) throw new Error("pollMs must be a positive safe integer");
+  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(klinePollers) || klinePollers < 0) throw new Error("klinePollers must be a non-negative safe integer");
+  const target = Math.floor(budgetPerMinute * PLANNED_SHARE);
+  const cost = (cadenceMs) => klinePollers * Math.ceil(MINUTE_MS / cadenceMs) * REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES + REQUEST_WEIGHTS.SERVER_TIME;
+  let klineCadenceMs = pollMs;
+  let stretched = false;
+  for (let multiple = 2; cost(klineCadenceMs) > target && multiple <= MAX_CADENCE_STRETCH; multiple += 1) {
+    klineCadenceMs = pollMs * multiple;
+    stretched = true;
+  }
+  return Object.freeze({
+    klineCadenceMs,
+    stretched,
+    projectedPerMinute: cost(klineCadenceMs),
+    fits: cost(klineCadenceMs) <= target,
+  });
+}
+
+module.exports = {
+  createBinanceRestWeightBudget,
+  planCadence,
+  usedWeightFromHeaders,
+  REQUEST_WEIGHTS,
+  EXCHANGE_WEIGHT_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE,
+  DEFAULT_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE,
+  MAX_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE,
+  MIN_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE,
+  USED_WEIGHT_HEADER,
+};
