+const { createBinanceRestBarCursor, DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES } = require("../../../src/providers/binance/binance-rest-bar-cursor");
+const { parseKlineRows } = require("../../../src/providers/binance/binance-event-normalizer");
+
+const MINUTE = 60_000;
+const T0 = 1_800_000_000_000 - (1_800_000_000_000 % MINUTE);
+
+function rawRow(openTime, { open = "64200.00", high = "64300.50", low = "64150.25", close = "64275.99", volume = "12.3", trades = 418, takerBuy = "6.0" } = {}) {
+  return [openTime, open, high, low, close, volume, openTime + MINUTE - 1, "792345.11", trades, takerBuy, "385000.0", "0"];
+}
+
+function rows(...raws) {
+  const parsed = parseKlineRows(raws, MINUTE);
+  if (parsed === null) throw new Error("test rows must parse");
+  return parsed;
+}
+
+describe("binance-rest-bar-cursor", () => {
+  test("classifies every row by its own close time against the exchange clock, not by position: the previous bar is final, the current forms", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, finalizeGraceMs: 1000 });
+    const exchangeNow = T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 30_000;
+    expect(cursor.nextRequest(exchangeNow)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + MINUTE, limit: 2, catchUp: false });
+    const outcome = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: exchangeNow, seq: 1 });
+    expect(outcome.stale).toBe(false);
+    expect(outcome.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[MINUTE, true], [2 * MINUTE, false]]);
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0 + MINUTE);
+  });
+
+  test("a revision replaces the same forming bar only when it changed; finalisation happens once, with the grace after the close", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, finalizeGraceMs: 1000 });
+    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 10_000, seq: 1 });
+    const unchanged = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 11_000, seq: 2 });
+    expect(unchanged.emit).toEqual([]);
+    const revised = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64280.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 12_000, seq: 3 });
+    expect(revised.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed, row.close])).toEqual([[MINUTE, false, "64280.00"]]);
+    // Exactly at the close the bar is still forming (the grace has not passed); one second later it is final, once.
+    const atClose = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64281.00" }), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 500, seq: 4 });
+    expect(atClose.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[MINUTE, false], [2 * MINUTE, false]]);
+    const afterGrace = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64281.00" }), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 1000, seq: 5 });
+    expect(afterGrace.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[MINUTE, true]]);
+    const again = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64281.00" }), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 2000, seq: 6 });
+    expect(again.emit).toEqual([]);
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0 + MINUTE);
+  });
+
+  test("the forming-bar change detector compares every downstream field: open, high, low, close, volume, taker-buy volume and trade count", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
+    const at = T0 + MINUTE + 10_000;
+    let seq = 1;
+    const emitForming = (overrides) => cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, overrides)), exchangeNowMs: at, seq: seq++ }).emit
+      .filter(({ closed }) => !closed).length;
+    expect(emitForming({})).toBe(1);
+    expect(emitForming({})).toBe(0);
+    for (const change of [
+      { open: "64201.00" },
+      { high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
+      { low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
+      { close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
+      { volume: "12.4", close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
+      { takerBuy: "6.1", volume: "12.4", close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
+      { trades: 419, takerBuy: "6.1", volume: "12.4", close: "64280.00", low: "64100.00", high: "64301.00", open: "64201.00" },
+    ]) {
+      expect(emitForming(change)).toBe(1);
+      expect(emitForming(change)).toBe(0);
+    }
+  });
+
+  test("a response from an older poll never overwrites a newer revision", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
+    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64280.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 5000, seq: 2 });
+    const late = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64270.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 6000, seq: 1 });
+    expect(late.stale).toBe(true);
+    expect(late.emit).toEqual([]);
+    const next = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE, { close: "64280.00" })), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 7000, seq: 3 });
+    expect(next.emit).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  test("clock skew: a machine clock ahead of the exchange cannot finalise a bar the exchange still has open", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, finalizeGraceMs: 1000 });
+    const machineNow = T0 + 2 * MINUTE + 20_000;
+    const exchangeNow = machineNow - 30_000; // the machine runs 30 s ahead
+    expect(cursor.nextRequest(exchangeNow)).toEqual({ startTime: T0, limit: 2, catchUp: false });
+    const outcome = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: exchangeNow, seq: 1 });
+    // By the machine clock the T0+1m bar closed 20 s ago; by the exchange it has 10 s to run.
+    expect(outcome.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed])).toEqual([[0, true], [MINUTE, false]]);
+  });
+
+  test("after a multi-minute outage it catches up from the last confirmed close in bounded pages before the two-row poll resumes", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
+    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(T0 + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 5000, seq: 1 });
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0);
+    const afterOutage = T0 + 6 * MINUTE + 5000;
+    expect(cursor.needsCatchUp(afterOutage)).toBe(true);
+    expect(cursor.nextRequest(afterOutage)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + MINUTE, limit: 1000, catchUp: true });
+    const page = cursor.apply({
+      rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 3 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 4 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 5 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 6 * MINUTE)),
+      exchangeNowMs: afterOutage,
+      seq: 2,
+    });
+    expect(page.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime / MINUTE - T0 / MINUTE, closed])).toEqual([[1, true], [2, true], [3, true], [4, true], [5, true], [6, false]]);
+    expect(page.hole).toBeNull();
+    expect(page.catchingUp).toBe(false);
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0 + 5 * MINUTE);
+    expect(cursor.nextRequest(afterOutage)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + 5 * MINUTE, limit: 2, catchUp: false });
+    // A page that stops short keeps the catch-up going.
+    const stillBehind = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
+    stillBehind.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + MINUTE + 5000, seq: 1 });
+    const partial = stillBehind.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 2 });
+    expect(partial.catchingUp).toBe(true);
+    expect(stillBehind.nextRequest(T0 + 10 * MINUTE)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + 3 * MINUTE, limit: 1000, catchUp: true });
+  });
+
+  test("finality is judged at dispatch: a row asked for before the close stays forming even when received after it, and the later poll's values are the final ones", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, finalizeGraceMs: 1000 });
+    const P = T0 + MINUTE;
+    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(P, { close: "64270.00" })), exchangeNowMs: P + 30_000, seq: 1 });
+    // Dispatched half a second before P closes; the exchange built the row then, whatever the receipt time.
+    const early = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0), rawRow(P, { close: "64275.00" })), exchangeNowMs: P + MINUTE - 500, seq: 2 });
+    expect(early.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed, row.close])).toEqual([[MINUTE, false, "64275.00"]]);
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0);
+    // The next poll, dispatched after the close plus grace, carries the true final values.
+    const late = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(P, { close: "64281.00" }), rawRow(P + MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: P + MINUTE + 1500, seq: 3 });
+    expect(late.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [row.openTime - T0, closed, row.close])).toEqual([[MINUTE, true, "64281.00"], [2 * MINUTE, false, "64275.99"]]);
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(P);
+  });
+
+  test("a recoverable hole: a page starting past the continuation keeps the cursor on the first missing minute and asks again until the exchange supplies it", () => {
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, maxHoleRetries: 3 });
+    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 1 });
+    const withHole = () => rows(rawRow(T0 + 3 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 4 * MINUTE));
+    const first = cursor.apply({ rows: withHole(), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 2 });
+    expect(first.hole).toEqual({ fromOpenTime: T0 + MINUTE, toOpenTime: T0 + 2 * MINUTE });
+    expect(first.emit).toEqual([]);
+    expect(first.repairing).toBe(true);
+    expect(first.catchingUp).toBe(true);
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0);
+    expect(cursor.nextRequest(T0 + 10 * MINUTE)).toEqual({ startTime: T0 + MINUTE, limit: 1000, catchUp: true });
+    const second = cursor.apply({ rows: withHole(), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 3 });
+    expect(second.hole).not.toBeNull();
+    expect(second.abandoned).toBeNull();
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0);
+    const supplied = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 2 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 3 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 4 });
+    expect(supplied.hole).toBeNull();
+    expect(supplied.repairing).toBe(false);
+    expect(supplied.emit.map(({ row }) => (row.openTime - T0) / MINUTE)).toEqual([1, 2, 3]);
+    expect(cursor.missing).toEqual([]);
+    expect(cursor.missingBars()).toBe(0);
+    expect(cursor.stats).toMatchObject({ holes: 1, holeRetries: 0, missingBars: 0 });
+  });
+
+  test("an irrecoverable hole: after the bounded retries the interval is recorded as missing, the cursor moves past it and the candles the exchange has are delivered", () => {
+    expect(DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES).toBe(3);
+    const cursor = createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE });
+    cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 1 });
+    const withHole = () => rows(rawRow(T0 + 3 * MINUTE), rawRow(T0 + 4 * MINUTE));
+    expect(cursor.apply({ rows: withHole(), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 2 }).abandoned).toBeNull();
+    expect(cursor.apply({ rows: withHole(), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 3 }).abandoned).toBeNull();
+    const third = cursor.apply({ rows: withHole(), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 4 });
+    expect(third.abandoned).toEqual({ fromOpenTime: T0 + MINUTE, toOpenTime: T0 + 2 * MINUTE });
+    expect(third.repairing).toBe(false);
+    expect(third.emit.map(({ row, closed }) => [(row.openTime - T0) / MINUTE, closed])).toEqual([[3, true], [4, true]]);
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBe(T0 + 4 * MINUTE);
+    expect(cursor.missing).toEqual([{ fromOpenTime: T0 + MINUTE, toOpenTime: T0 + 2 * MINUTE }]);
+    expect(cursor.missingBars()).toBe(2);
+    expect(cursor.stats).toMatchObject({ holes: 1, missingBars: 2 });
+    // A different hole later starts its own retry count.
+    const other = cursor.apply({ rows: rows(rawRow(T0 + 7 * MINUTE)), exchangeNowMs: T0 + 10 * MINUTE, seq: 5 });
+    expect(other.hole).toEqual({ fromOpenTime: T0 + 5 * MINUTE, toOpenTime: T0 + 6 * MINUTE });
+    expect(other.abandoned).toBeNull();
+    expect(cursor.stats.holes).toBe(2);
+    cursor.reset();
+    expect(cursor.confirmedOpenTime).toBeNull();
+    expect(cursor.repairing).toBeNull();
+    expect(() => createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: 0 })).toThrow(/intervalMs/);
+    expect(() => createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs: MINUTE, maxHoleRetries: 0 })).toThrow(/maxHoleRetries/);
+  });
+});

+const {
+  createBinanceRestConnection,
+  binanceTransportFromEnv,
+  HEALTH_REASON_BASE,
+  BOOK_UNAVAILABLE_REASON,
+  historyIncompleteVariant,
+  DEFAULT_MAX_CATCH_UP_PAGES,
+  DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS,
+} = require("../../../src/providers/binance/binance-rest-connection");
+const { normalizeBinanceEvent } = require("../../../src/providers/binance/binance-event-normalizer");
+const { createInstrumentTickSizeResolver } = require("../../../src/providers/shared/instrument-tick-size");
+
+const MINUTE = 60_000;
+const T0 = 1_800_000_000_000 - (1_800_000_000_000 % MINUTE) + 30_000; // :30 into a minute
+const OPEN = Math.floor(T0 / MINUTE) * MINUTE;
+const SPOT_TRADE = { symbol: "BTCUSDT", marketKind: "SPOT", eventType: "TRADE" };
+const SPOT_KLINE = { symbol: "BTCUSDT", marketKind: "SPOT", eventType: "KLINE", interval: "1m" };
+const SPOT_DEPTH = { symbol: "BTCUSDT", marketKind: "SPOT", eventType: "DEPTH" };
+const ETH_KLINE = { symbol: "ETHUSDT", marketKind: "SPOT", eventType: "KLINE", interval: "1m" };
+
+function klineRow(openTime, { close = "64275.99", volume = "12.3", trades = 418, takerBuy = "6.0" } = {}) {
+  return [openTime, "64200.00", "64300.50", "64150.25", close, volume, openTime + MINUTE - 1, "792345.11", trades, takerBuy, "385000.0", "0"];
+}
+
+/**
+ * A scripted Binance: candles are derived from the fake clock, so the same
+ * handler serves the two-row poll and catch-up pages; exchangeInfo and time
+ * answer with the fake clock plus a configurable skew.
+ */
+function fakeBinance() {
+  const state = {
+    serverTimeOffsetMs: 0,
+    usedWeight: 10,
+    klineStart: OPEN - 10 * MINUTE,
+    rowFor: () => ({}),
+    omit: new Set(),
+    failures: [],
+    hang: null,
+    pending: [],
+  };
+  const requests = [];
+  function respond(body, status = 200, headers = {}) {
+    return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": String(state.usedWeight), ...headers } });
+  }
+  function currentOpen() {
+    return Math.floor((Date.now() + state.serverTimeOffsetMs) / MINUTE) * MINUTE;
+  }
+  const fetchImpl = jest.fn(async (url, init) => {
+    const u = new URL(url);
+    requests.push(u);
+    const failure = state.failures.find((entry) => entry.match(u) && entry.times > 0);
+    if (failure) {
+      failure.times -= 1;
+      if (failure.network) throw new TypeError("fetch failed");
+      return respond(failure.body ?? {}, failure.status, failure.headers ?? {});
+    }
+    if (state.hang && state.hang(u)) {
+      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+        init.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
+          const error = new Error("aborted");
+          error.name = "AbortError";
+          reject(error);
+        });
+        state.pending.push((body) => resolve(respond(body)));
+      });
+    }
+    switch (u.pathname) {
+      case "/api/v3/exchangeInfo":
+        return respond({
+          serverTime: Date.now() + state.serverTimeOffsetMs,
+          symbols: [{ symbol: u.searchParams.get("symbol"), filters: [{ filterType: "PRICE_FILTER", tickSize: "0.01000000" }] }],
+        });
+      case "/api/v3/time":
+        return respond({ serverTime: Date.now() + state.serverTimeOffsetMs });
+      case "/api/v3/klines": {
+        const startTime = Number(u.searchParams.get("startTime"));
+        const limit = Number(u.searchParams.get("limit"));
+        const rows = [];
+        for (let openTime = Math.max(startTime, state.klineStart); openTime <= currentOpen() && rows.length < limit; openTime += MINUTE) {
+          if (state.omit.has(openTime)) continue;
+          rows.push(klineRow(openTime, state.rowFor(openTime)));
+        }
+        return respond(rows);
+      }
+      default:
+        return respond({}, 404);
+    }
+  });
+  return {
+    fetchImpl,
+    requests,
+    state,
+    pathsRequested: () => requests.map((u) => u.pathname),
+    klineRequests: () => requests.filter((u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines"),
+  };
+}
+
+const active = new Set();
+
+function connection(fake, options = {}) {
+  const created = createBinanceRestConnection({ fetchImpl: fake.fetchImpl, random: () => 0.5, telemetry: () => {}, ...options });
+  active.add(created);
+  return created;
+}
+
+async function connected(fake, options) {
+  const created = connection(fake, options);
+  created.connect();
+  await tick(0);
+  expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+  return created;
+}
+
+// Advance the fake clock, then let the fetch and body-reading promise chains
+// settle: a poll completing at the end of the window schedules its follow-up
+// a few microtask turns later, and a zero-delay timer created during a fake
+// tick is due one millisecond on, so the flush steps by one millisecond.
+async function tick(ms) {
+  await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(ms);
+  for (let turn = 0; turn < 6; turn += 1) await jest.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1);
+}
+
+function collector(created) {
+  const envelopes = [];
+  created.onMessage((envelope) => envelopes.push(envelope));
+  return envelopes;
+}
+
+function bars(envelopes) {
+  return envelopes.map((e) => [(e.payload.openTime - OPEN) / MINUTE, e.payload.isClosed]);
+}
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  jest.useFakeTimers({ doNotFake: ["nextTick"] });
+  jest.setSystemTime(T0);
+});
+
+afterEach(() => {
+  for (const created of active) created.disconnect();
+  active.clear();
+  jest.useRealTimers();
+});
+
+describe("binance-rest-connection: route, clock and subscriptions", () => {
+  test("binanceTransportFromEnv defaults to ws, accepts rest, refuses anything else", () => {
+    expect(binanceTransportFromEnv({})).toBe("ws");
+    expect(binanceTransportFromEnv({ TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT: " " })).toBe("ws");
+    expect(binanceTransportFromEnv({ TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT: "rest" })).toBe("rest");
+    expect(() => binanceTransportFromEnv({ TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT: "http" })).toThrow(/ws or rest/);
+  });
+
+  test("connect() probes exchangeInfo (weight 20) for the route and the exchange clock; PERPETUAL routes stay DISCONNECTED and cannot be subscribed", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    fake.state.serverTimeOffsetMs = 1500;
+    const created = connection(fake);
+    expect(created.getStatus()).toEqual({ SPOT: { public: "DISCONNECTED" }, PERPETUAL: { public: "DISCONNECTED", market: "DISCONNECTED" } });
+    expect(() => created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1")).toThrow(/Cannot subscribe before the Binance SPOT public route is connected/);
+    created.connect();
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTING");
+    await tick(0);
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+    expect(fake.pathsRequested()).toEqual(["/api/v3/exchangeInfo"]);
+    expect(() => created.subscribe({ symbol: "BTCUSDT", marketKind: "PERPETUAL", eventType: "KLINE", interval: "1m" }, "c1"))
+      .toThrow(/Cannot subscribe before the Binance PERPETUAL public route is connected/);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("PERPETUAL", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toBeUndefined();
+    const stats = created.getOperationalStats();
+    expect(stats).toMatchObject({ transport: "rest", routeState: "CONNECTED", probes: 1, clockSyncs: 1, exchangeOffsetMs: 1500 });
+    expect(stats.weight.used).toBe(20);
+  });
+
+  test("the exchange clock is refreshed from /api/v3/time once a minute, charged 1 to the budget, and a failed refresh keeps the last offset", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    await tick(DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS);
+    expect(fake.pathsRequested()).toEqual(["/api/v3/exchangeInfo", "/api/v3/time"]);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats()).toMatchObject({ clockSyncs: 2, exchangeOffsetMs: 0 });
+    fake.state.serverTimeOffsetMs = -2000;
+    await tick(DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats()).toMatchObject({ clockSyncs: 3, exchangeOffsetMs: -2000 });
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/time", times: 1, status: 503 });
+    await tick(DEFAULT_CLOCK_REFRESH_MS);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats()).toMatchObject({ clockSyncs: 3, clockRefreshFailures: 1, exchangeOffsetMs: -2000, routeState: "CONNECTED" });
+    // 20 for the probe plus one per refresh minute: the minute roll resets the count, so only this minute's refresh shows.
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().weight.reserved).toBe(1);
+  });
+
+  test("the tape and the book are accepted but unavailable: no trade frames, book UNAVAILABLE with reason REST_NO_DEPTH, health flagged unserved", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    expect(created.getOrderBook("SPOT", "BTCUSDT")).toBeUndefined();
+    created.subscribeMany([SPOT_TRADE, SPOT_KLINE, SPOT_DEPTH], "server");
+    expect(created.getOrderBook("SPOT", "BTCUSDT")).toEqual({ status: "UNAVAILABLE", reason: BOOK_UNAVAILABLE_REASON });
+    expect(created.getOrderBookStatus("SPOT", "BTCUSDT")).toEqual({ status: "UNAVAILABLE", reason: "REST_NO_DEPTH" });
+    expect(created.getDepthSnapshot("SPOT", "BTCUSDT")).toBeUndefined();
+    for (const [eventType, interval] of [["TRADE", undefined], ["DEPTH", undefined]]) {
+      expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", eventType, interval)).toEqual({
+        stale: true, recoveryState: "UNAVAILABLE", transport: "rest", served: false, reason: HEALTH_REASON_BASE,
+      });
+    }
+    expect(created.recoverStaleSubscription(SPOT_DEPTH)).toBe(false);
+    expect(created.recoverStaleSubscription(SPOT_TRADE)).toBe(false);
+    await tick(3000);
+    expect(fake.pathsRequested().filter((path) => path !== "/api/v3/klines" && path !== "/api/v3/exchangeInfo")).toEqual([]);
+    expect(envelopes.every((e) => e.eventType === "KLINE")).toBe(true);
+    expect(envelopes.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: false, recoveryState: "READY", served: true, reason: HEALTH_REASON_BASE, pollMs: 1000 });
+  });
+
+  test("unsubscribing the last holder cancels the in-flight request and a response that lands afterwards is thrown away", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    fake.state.hang = (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines";
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c2");
+    await tick(0);
+    expect(fake.state.pending).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().inflight).toBe(1);
+    expect(created.unsubscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1")).toBe(false);
+    expect(created.unsubscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c2")).toBe(true);
+    fake.state.pending[0]([klineRow(OPEN - MINUTE), klineRow(OPEN)]);
+    await tick(5000);
+    expect(envelopes).toEqual([]);
+    expect(fake.klineRequests()).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().inflight).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  test("removeConsumer releases every subscription of a consumer and quiesce resolves once nothing is in flight", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    created.subscribeMany([SPOT_TRADE, SPOT_KLINE, SPOT_DEPTH], "server");
+    await tick(10);
+    expect(created.removeConsumer("server").map((d) => d.eventType).sort()).toEqual(["DEPTH", "KLINE", "TRADE"]);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().pollers).toEqual([]);
+    const settled = created.quiesce({ timeoutMs: 1000 });
+    await tick(20);
+    await expect(settled).resolves.toBeUndefined();
+  });
+});
+
+describe("binance-rest-connection: candles", () => {
+  test("polls the previous and forming bar: a revision only when a field changed, the close emitted once after the grace, with the WebSocket kline envelope shape", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    expect(bars(envelopes)).toEqual([[-1, true], [0, false]]);
+    expect(envelopes[1]).toMatchObject({ stale: false, recoveryState: "READY" });
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(envelopes).toHaveLength(2); // unchanged forming bar: no revision
+    fake.state.rowFor = (openTime) => (openTime === OPEN ? { takerBuy: "6.5" } : {});
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(envelopes.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ payload: { openTime: OPEN, isClosed: false, takerBuyBaseVolume: "6.5" } });
+    jest.setSystemTime(OPEN + MINUTE + 300);
+    await tick(400); // 700 ms after the close: the grace has not passed on the exchange clock
+    expect(envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.openTime === OPEN && e.payload.isClosed)).toHaveLength(0);
+    await tick(1000);
+    const closed = envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.openTime === OPEN && e.payload.isClosed);
+    expect(closed).toHaveLength(1);
+    await tick(3000);
+    expect(envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.openTime === OPEN && e.payload.isClosed)).toHaveLength(1);
+    const viaStream = normalizeBinanceEvent(
+      { marketKind: "SPOT", symbol: "BTCUSDT", eventType: "KLINE", interval: "1m", raw: { e: "kline", E: T0, s: "BTCUSDT", k: { t: OPEN, T: OPEN + MINUTE - 1, s: "BTCUSDT", i: "1m", f: 1, L: 2, o: "64200.00", c: "64275.99", h: "64300.50", l: "64150.25", v: "12.3", n: 418, x: true, q: "792345.11", V: "6.5", Q: "385000.0", B: "0" } } },
+      { receivedAtMs: T0 },
+    );
+    expect(Object.keys(closed[0]).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(viaStream).sort());
+    expect(Object.keys(closed[0].payload).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(viaStream.payload).sort());
+    expect(closed[0].payload).toEqual(viaStream.payload);
+    expect(closed[0]).toMatchObject({ provider: "BINANCE", venue: "BINANCE", marketKind: "SPOT", providerSymbol: "BTCUSDT", eventType: "KLINE", timingClass: "REALTIME", stale: false, recoveryState: "READY" });
+    expect(Object.isFrozen(closed[0])).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  test("clock skew: with the machine 30 s ahead of the exchange, a bar the exchange still has open is never finalised early", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    fake.state.serverTimeOffsetMs = -45_000; // the machine is :30 into a minute, the exchange :45 into the previous one
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    const exchangeOpen = OPEN - MINUTE; // the exchange is still in the previous minute
+    expect(envelopes.map((e) => [(e.payload.openTime - exchangeOpen) / MINUTE, e.payload.isClosed])).toEqual([[-1, true], [0, false]]);
+    jest.setSystemTime(exchangeOpen + MINUTE + 20_000); // closed by the machine 20 s ago, open on the exchange for 25 more seconds
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.openTime === exchangeOpen && e.payload.isClosed)).toHaveLength(0);
+    jest.setSystemTime(exchangeOpen + MINUTE + 47_000); // two seconds past the close on the exchange: the grace has passed
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.openTime === exchangeOpen && e.payload.isClosed)).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().exchangeOffsetMs).toBe(-45_000);
+  });
+
+  test("a multi-minute outage: polls fail, the feed goes stale then UNAVAILABLE, and after it catch-up emits every missed close in order before the two-row poll resumes", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    // The whole origin is unreachable: polls and probes fail alike.
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: () => true, times: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, network: true });
+    await tick(2000);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: true, recoveryState: "RECOVERING", reason: `${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · poll failed` });
+    await tick(5 * MINUTE);
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("DISCONNECTED");
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: true, recoveryState: "UNAVAILABLE", reason: `${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · poll failed` });
+    const emittedBeforeOutage = envelopes.length;
+    fake.state.failures.length = 0;
+    await tick(31_000); // the probe retries at most every 30 s
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+    await tick(2000); // one steady two-row poll after the catch-up
+    const currentBar = Math.floor(Date.now() / MINUTE) - OPEN / MINUTE;
+    const closes = envelopes.slice(emittedBeforeOutage).filter((e) => e.payload.isClosed).map((e) => (e.payload.openTime - OPEN) / MINUTE);
+    expect(closes).toEqual(Array.from({ length: currentBar }, (_, i) => i));
+    expect(currentBar).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5);
+    // The current bar was announced forming (it may since have been followed by the previous bar's close after its grace).
+    expect(envelopes.some((e) => e.payload.openTime === OPEN + currentBar * MINUTE && e.payload.isClosed === false)).toBe(true);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: false, recoveryState: "READY", reason: HEALTH_REASON_BASE });
+    const catchUpRequest = fake.klineRequests().find((u) => u.searchParams.get("limit") === "1000");
+    expect(Number(catchUpRequest.searchParams.get("startTime"))).toBe(OPEN); // continuation cursor: the confirmed close plus one bar
+    expect(fake.klineRequests().at(-1).searchParams.get("limit")).toBe("2");
+  });
+
+  test("catch-up pages hold 1000 candles, continue from the confirmed close, run at most maxCatchUpPages back to back, and read catching up while they run", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    fake.state.klineStart = OPEN - 5000 * MINUTE;
+    const telemetry = jest.fn();
+    const created = await connected(fake, { maxCatchUpPages: 2, telemetry });
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    expect(DEFAULT_MAX_CATCH_UP_PAGES).toBe(10);
+    // A long silence: the next poll finds itself 2500 bars behind.
+    jest.setSystemTime(T0 + 2500 * MINUTE);
+    await tick(1000);
+    const pages = fake.klineRequests().filter((u) => u.searchParams.get("limit") === "1000");
+    expect(pages.map((u) => (Number(u.searchParams.get("startTime")) - OPEN) / MINUTE)).toEqual([0, 1000]);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ recoveryState: "RECOVERING", stale: true, reason: `${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · catching up` });
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().pollers[0]).toMatchObject({ catchingUp: true, confirmedOpenTime: OPEN + 1999 * MINUTE });
+    expect(telemetry.mock.calls.some(([entry]) => entry.event === "market_data.binance_rest.kline_catch_up_started")).toBe(true);
+    await tick(1000); // the poller yielded for one cadence, then continues
+    const morePages = fake.klineRequests().filter((u) => u.searchParams.get("limit") === "1000");
+    expect(morePages.map((u) => (Number(u.searchParams.get("startTime")) - OPEN) / MINUTE)).toEqual([0, 1000, 2000]);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ recoveryState: "READY", reason: HEALTH_REASON_BASE });
+    expect(telemetry.mock.calls.some(([entry]) => entry.event === "market_data.binance_rest.kline_catch_up_completed")).toBe(true);
+    const closes = envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.isClosed).map((e) => (e.payload.openTime - OPEN) / MINUTE);
+    expect(closes).toEqual(Array.from({ length: 2501 }, (_, i) => i - 1)); // bars -1 .. 2499 closed; 2500 is forming
+    await tick(1000); // back on the steady two-row poll
+    expect(fake.klineRequests().at(-1).searchParams.get("limit")).toBe("2");
+  });
+});

+describe("binance-rest-connection: honesty under failure and the weight budget", () => {
+  test("429 pauses every poll for Retry-After and says rate limited; a 418 stops polling for its ban window and takes the route down until a probe succeeds", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    created.subscribeMany([SPOT_KLINE, ETH_KLINE], "server");
+    await tick(0);
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines", times: 1, status: 429, headers: { "retry-after": "3", "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": "3900" } });
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m").reason).toBe(`${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · rate limited`);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "ETHUSDT", "KLINE", "1m").reason).toBe(`${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · rate limited`);
+    // Retry-After 3 s from the failed poll, honoured over the 1 s backoff
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().rateLimitedUntilMs).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(Date.now() + 2950);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().rateLimitedUntilMs).toBeLessThanOrEqual(Date.now() + 3000);
+    const requestsAtLimit = fake.requests.length;
+    await tick(2500);
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBe(requestsAtLimit); // nothing is sent inside the Retry-After window
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().weight.observed).toBe(3900); // the exchange's count is adopted
+    await tick(1500);
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBeGreaterThan(requestsAtLimit);
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines", times: 1, status: 418, headers: { "retry-after": "10" } });
+    await tick(2000);
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("DISCONNECTED");
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: true, recoveryState: "UNAVAILABLE" });
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m").reason).toContain("rate limited");
+    const requestsAtBan = fake.requests.length;
+    await tick(8500); // inside the 10 s ban window (the fake clock drifts a few ms per tick)
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBe(requestsAtBan);
+    await tick(2500);
+    expect(fake.pathsRequested().slice(requestsAtBan)[0]).toBe("/api/v3/exchangeInfo");
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: false, recoveryState: "READY", reason: HEALTH_REASON_BASE });
+  });
+
+  test("three transport failures in a row take the route down until a probe succeeds; a failed poll emits nothing and reads poll failed, never a quiet market", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const telemetry = jest.fn();
+    const created = await connected(fake, { telemetry });
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    const emittedAtStart = envelopes.length;
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines", times: 3, status: 503 });
+    fake.state.rowFor = () => ({ close: "64290.00" });
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(envelopes.length).toBe(emittedAtStart);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: true, recoveryState: "RECOVERING", reason: `${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · poll failed` });
+    await tick(10_000);
+    const routeEvents = telemetry.mock.calls.map(([entry]) => entry).filter((entry) => entry.event.startsWith("market_data.binance_rest.route_"));
+    expect(routeEvents.map((entry) => [entry.event.slice("market_data.binance_rest.".length), entry.reason ?? null])).toEqual([
+      ["route_connected", null],
+      ["route_disconnected", "HTTP_ERROR"],
+      ["route_connected", null],
+    ]);
+    expect(fake.pathsRequested().filter((path) => path === "/api/v3/exchangeInfo")).toHaveLength(2);
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+    expect(envelopes.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ payload: { openTime: OPEN, isClosed: false, close: "64290.00" }, recoveryState: "READY" });
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: false, recoveryState: "READY", reason: HEALTH_REASON_BASE });
+  });
+
+  test("weight is reserved before dispatch: a budget too small for the pairs lengthens the cadence and the reason says slow polling", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake, { weightBudgetPerMinute: 100 });
+    created.subscribeMany([SPOT_KLINE, ETH_KLINE], "server");
+    const stats = created.getOperationalStats();
+    expect(stats).toMatchObject({ klineCadenceMs: 3000, cadenceStretched: true, projectedWeightPerMinute: 81 });
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ reason: `${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · slow polling (weight budget)`, pollMs: 3000 });
+    await tick(MINUTE);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().weight.used).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
+  });
+
+  test("a 429 whose header says the whole limit is spent stops every poll until the minute turns, even after Retry-After", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines", times: 1, status: 429, headers: { "retry-after": "1", "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": "6010" } });
+    await tick(1000);
+    const sent = fake.requests.length;
+    await tick(20_000);
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBe(sent);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m").reason).toBe(`${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · slow polling (weight budget)`);
+    await tick(MINUTE);
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBeGreaterThan(sent);
+  });
+
+  test("when the exchange reports the budget already spent, concurrent polls are deferred to the next minute instead of sent", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake, { weightBudgetPerMinute: 100 });
+    created.subscribeMany([SPOT_KLINE, ETH_KLINE], "server");
+    await tick(0);

+    await tick(5000);

+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().budgetDeferrals).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().weight.used).toBeLessThanOrEqual(100);
+    fake.state.usedWeight = 10;
+    await tick(MINUTE);
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBeGreaterThan(sent);
+  });
+
+  test("the candle backfill goes through the same budget and reads the exchange's count back", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();

+    expect(result.rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().weight.reserved).toBe(22);
+  });
+});

+describe("binance-rest-connection: one dispatch gate for every request", () => {
+  const BACKFILL = { interval: "1m", intervalMs: MINUTE, days: 1 / 24 / 12 }; // five minutes
+
+  test("backfill and live polling together with the machine clock 45 s ahead: neither stores the exchange's forming candle as closed", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    fake.state.serverTimeOffsetMs = -45_000; // the machine is :30 into a minute, the exchange :45 into the previous one
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    const exchangeOpen = OPEN - MINUTE;
+    expect(envelopes.map((e) => [(e.payload.openTime - exchangeOpen) / MINUTE, e.payload.isClosed])).toEqual([[-1, true], [0, false]]);
+    const result = await created.fetchRecentKlines("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", BACKFILL);
+    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
+    expect(result.rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    // The newest history row is the bar before the exchange's forming bar, not the one the machine clock says has closed.
+    expect(result.rows.at(-1).openTime).toBe(exchangeOpen - MINUTE);
+    expect(result.rows.some((row) => row.openTime === exchangeOpen)).toBe(false);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().weight.reserved).toBe(20 + 2 + 2); // probe, one poll, one backfill page
+  });
+
+  test("a polling ban blocks the backfill: its pages wait at the gate until the ban window ends", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines", times: 1, status: 418, headers: { "retry-after": "10" } });
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("DISCONNECTED");
+    const requestsAtBan = fake.requests.length;
+    const pending = created.fetchRecentKlines("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", BACKFILL);
+    await tick(8000);
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBe(requestsAtBan);
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().gatedRequests).toBe(1);
+    await tick(3000);
+    const result = await pending;
+    expect(result.ok).toBe(true);
+    const afterBan = fake.requests.slice(requestsAtBan);
+    expect(afterBan[0].pathname).toBe("/api/v3/exchangeInfo"); // the probe reopened the route first
+    expect(afterBan.filter((u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines" && u.searchParams.get("limit") === "1000")).toHaveLength(1); // then the backfill page
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+  });
+
+  test("a backfill ban blocks polling: a 418 on a backfill page opens the ban window and takes the route down", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines" && u.searchParams.get("limit") === "1000", times: 1, status: 418, headers: { "retry-after": "10" } });
+    const result = await created.fetchRecentKlines("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", BACKFILL);
+    expect(result).toMatchObject({ ok: false, reason: "IP_BANNED", retryAfterMs: 10_000 });
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("DISCONNECTED");
+    const requestsAtBan = fake.requests.length;
+    await tick(8000);
+    expect(fake.requests.length).toBe(requestsAtBan);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: true, recoveryState: "UNAVAILABLE" });
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m").reason).toContain("rate limited");
+    await tick(3000);
+    expect(created.getStatus().SPOT.public).toBe("CONNECTED");
+    expect(fake.pathsRequested().slice(requestsAtBan)[0]).toBe("/api/v3/exchangeInfo");
+  });
+
+  test("a disconnect aborts a backfill queued at the gate instead of leaving it waiting", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    fake.state.failures.push({ match: (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines", times: 1, status: 418, headers: { "retry-after": "60" } });
+    await tick(1000);

+  test("clock refresh 429: the pause applies to polling and the last valid offset stays", async () => {

+    const sent = fake.requests.length;

+  });
+
+  test("clock refresh 418: the ban window opens, the route is down for it, and a probe brings it back", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();

+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");

+  });
+
+  test("a tick-size lookup while the budget is exhausted waits for the next minute and is charged 20", async () => {

+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");

+  test("a tick-size lookup during an IP ban waits at the gate until the ban ends", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();

+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);

+    await tick(1000);

+    const sent = fake.requests.length;
+    const lookup = resolver.getBinanceTickSize("SPOT", "BTCUSDT");

+    await expect(created.fetchExchangeInfo("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", { signal: {} })).rejects.toThrow(/AbortSignal/);
+  });
+});
+
+describe("binance-rest-connection: finality at dispatch and holes", () => {
+  test("a delayed response spanning the minute boundary never finalises the candle; the later poll's final values are the ones delivered", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const created = await connected(fake);
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    // Half a second before OPEN closes, the poll goes out and hangs on the way back.
+    jest.setSystemTime(OPEN + MINUTE - 500);
+    fake.state.hang = (u) => u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines";
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(fake.state.pending).toHaveLength(1);
+    fake.state.hang = null;
+    // Meanwhile the exchange has the final candle; the delayed response still carries the row it built before the close.
+    fake.state.rowFor = (openTime) => (openTime === OPEN ? { close: "64281.00", volume: "11.4" } : {});
+    jest.setSystemTime(OPEN + MINUTE + 1500);
+    const emittedBeforeLanding = envelopes.length;
+    fake.state.pending[0]([klineRow(OPEN - MINUTE), klineRow(OPEN, { close: "64270.00", volume: "11.0" })]);
+    await tick(0);
+    // The delayed row was dispatched before the close: a forming revision, never a close.
+    expect(envelopes[emittedBeforeLanding].payload).toMatchObject({ openTime: OPEN, isClosed: false, close: "64270.00", baseVolume: "11.0" });
+    await tick(1000);
+    const closed = envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.openTime === OPEN && e.payload.isClosed);
+    expect(closed).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(closed[0].payload).toMatchObject({ close: "64281.00", baseVolume: "11.4" });
+    expect(envelopes.some((e) => e.payload.openTime === OPEN && e.payload.isClosed && e.payload.close === "64270.00")).toBe(false);
+    // The close came from a poll dispatched after the close plus the grace.
+    const closeIndex = envelopes.indexOf(closed[0]);
+    expect(closeIndex).toBeGreaterThan(emittedBeforeLanding);
+  });
+
+  test("a recoverable hole: the poller stays degraded with the cursor on the first missing minute, and delivers the minutes in order once the exchange supplies them", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const telemetry = jest.fn();
+    const created = await connected(fake, { telemetry });
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    fake.state.omit.add(OPEN); // the exchange has no candle for OPEN yet
+    jest.setSystemTime(T0 + 3 * MINUTE);
+    await tick(1000);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: true, recoveryState: "RECOVERING", reason: `${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · catching up` });
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().pollers[0]).toMatchObject({ repairing: true, confirmedOpenTime: OPEN - MINUTE, missingBars: 0 });
+    expect(envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.isClosed && e.payload.openTime >= OPEN)).toEqual([]);
+    expect(telemetry.mock.calls.some(([entry]) => entry.event === "market_data.binance_rest.kline_hole_detected" && entry.fromOpenTime === OPEN && entry.toOpenTime === OPEN)).toBe(true);
+    fake.state.omit.delete(OPEN);
+    await tick(1000);
+    const closes = envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.isClosed).map((e) => (e.payload.openTime - OPEN) / MINUTE);
+    expect(closes).toEqual([-1, 0, 1, 2]);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({ stale: false, recoveryState: "READY", reason: HEALTH_REASON_BASE });
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().pollers[0]).toMatchObject({ repairing: false, missingBars: 0, confirmedOpenTime: OPEN + 2 * MINUTE });
+    expect(fake.klineRequests().filter((u) => Number(u.searchParams.get("startTime")) === OPEN && u.searchParams.get("limit") === "1000")).toHaveLength(2);
+  });
+
+  test("an irrecoverable hole: after bounded retries the missing minutes are reported on the health reason for the rest of the session, never as caught up", async () => {
+    const fake = fakeBinance();
+    const telemetry = jest.fn();
+    const created = await connected(fake, { telemetry });
+    const envelopes = collector(created);
+    created.subscribe(SPOT_KLINE, "c1");
+    await tick(0);
+    fake.state.omit.add(OPEN);
+    jest.setSystemTime(T0 + 3 * MINUTE);
+    await tick(1000); // attempt 1: hole, stay degraded
+    await tick(1000); // attempt 2
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m").recoveryState).toBe("RECOVERING");
+    await tick(1000); // attempt 3: given up
+    const closes = envelopes.filter((e) => e.payload.isClosed).map((e) => (e.payload.openTime - OPEN) / MINUTE);
+    expect(closes).toEqual([-1, 1, 2]);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m")).toMatchObject({
+      stale: false, recoveryState: "READY", reason: `${HEALTH_REASON_BASE} · ${historyIncompleteVariant(1)}`,
+    });
+    expect(historyIncompleteVariant(1)).toBe("history incomplete (1 min missing)");
+    expect(created.getOperationalStats().pollers[0]).toMatchObject({ repairing: false, missingBars: 1, confirmedOpenTime: OPEN + 2 * MINUTE });
+    expect(telemetry.mock.calls.some(([entry]) => entry.event === "market_data.binance_rest.kline_hole_abandoned" && entry.missingBars === 1)).toBe(true);
+    await tick(5000);
+    expect(created.getFeedHealth("SPOT", "BTCUSDT", "KLINE", "1m").reason).toContain("history incomplete (1 min missing)");
+    expect(fake.klineRequests().at(-1).searchParams.get("limit")).toBe("2");
+  });
+});

+const {
+  createBinanceRestWeightBudget,
+  planCadence,
+  usedWeightFromHeaders,
+  REQUEST_WEIGHTS,
+  DEFAULT_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE,
+  MAX_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE,
+  EXCHANGE_WEIGHT_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE,
+} = require("../../../src/providers/binance/binance-rest-weight-budget");
+
+const MINUTE_MS = 60_000;
+
+function fakeClock(startMs) {
+  let at = startMs;
+  return { now: () => at, advance(ms) { at += ms; }, set(ms) { at = ms; } };
+}
+
+describe("binance-rest-weight-budget", () => {
+  test("the corrected weights and the margin: default 4000, maximum 5000, exchange limit 6000", () => {
+    expect(REQUEST_WEIGHTS).toEqual({ KLINES: 2, EXCHANGE_INFO: 20, SERVER_TIME: 1 });
+    expect(DEFAULT_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE).toBe(4000);
+    expect(MAX_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE).toBe(5000);
+    expect(EXCHANGE_WEIGHT_LIMIT_PER_MINUTE - MAX_WEIGHT_BUDGET_PER_MINUTE).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1000);
+    expect(() => createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: 5001 })).toThrow(/between 100 and 5000/);
+    expect(() => createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: 99 })).toThrow(/between 100 and 5000/);
+    expect(() => createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: 4000.5 })).toThrow(/between 100 and 5000/);
+  });
+
+  test("weight is reserved before dispatch; a reservation that would cross the budget is refused and counted, never granted", () => {
+    const clock = fakeClock(1_800_000_000_000);
+    const budget = createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: 100, now: clock.now });
+    expect(budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.EXCHANGE_INFO)).toBe(true);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) expect(budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES)).toBe(true);
+    expect(budget.stats().used).toBe(100);
+    expect(budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.SERVER_TIME)).toBe(false);
+    expect(budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES)).toBe(false);
+    expect(budget.stats()).toMatchObject({ reserved: 100, used: 100, remaining: 0, deferrals: 2, reservations: 41 });
+    expect(() => budget.reserve(0)).toThrow(/positive/);
+  });
+
+  test("concurrent reservations in the same tick are granted first come first served up to the budget, and the rest wait for the minute", () => {
+    const clock = fakeClock(1_800_000_000_000);
+    const budget = createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: 100, now: clock.now });
+    const outcomes = Array.from({ length: 55 }, () => budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES));
+    expect(outcomes.filter(Boolean)).toHaveLength(50);
+    expect(outcomes.slice(50)).toEqual([false, false, false, false, false]);
+    expect(budget.msUntilReset()).toBe(MINUTE_MS - (1_800_000_000_000 % MINUTE_MS));
+    clock.advance(budget.msUntilReset());
+    expect(budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES)).toBe(true);
+    expect(budget.stats()).toMatchObject({ reserved: 2, deferrals: 5 });
+  });
+
+  test("a near-limit header permits exactly one further request: reservations keep adding on top of the exchange's count", () => {
+    const clock = fakeClock(1_800_000_000_000);
+    const budget = createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: 100, now: clock.now });
+    expect(budget.reserve(20)).toBe(true);
+    expect(budget.observe({ "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": "98" })).toBe(98);
+    expect(budget.used()).toBe(98);
+    expect(budget.reserve(2)).toBe(true);
+    expect(budget.used()).toBe(100);
+    expect(budget.reserve(2)).toBe(false);
+    expect(budget.reserve(1)).toBe(false);
+    expect(budget.stats()).toMatchObject({ reserved: 22, observed: 98, used: 100, remaining: 0, deferrals: 2 });
+    // A later, lower header never lowers the accounted total.
+    expect(budget.observe({ "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": "50" })).toBe(50);
+    expect(budget.used()).toBe(100);
+  });
+
+  test("the exchange's own x-mbx-used-weight-1m raises the count when it is higher and never lowers it", () => {
+    const clock = fakeClock(1_800_000_000_000);
+    const budget = createBinanceRestWeightBudget({ budgetPerMinute: 1000, now: clock.now });
+    budget.reserve(REQUEST_WEIGHTS.KLINES);
+    expect(budget.observe({ "X-MBX-USED-WEIGHT-1M": "950" })).toBe(950);
+    expect(budget.used()).toBe(950);
+    expect(budget.observe({ "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": "10" })).toBe(10);
+    expect(budget.used()).toBe(950);
+    expect(budget.reserve(60)).toBe(false);
+    expect(budget.reserve(50)).toBe(true);
+    expect(budget.observe({})).toBeNull();
+    expect(budget.observe(null)).toBeNull();
+    expect(usedWeightFromHeaders({ "x-mbx-used-weight-1m": "abc" })).toBeNull();
+    clock.set(1_800_000_060_000);
+    expect(budget.used()).toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  test("planCadence keeps the candle polls inside 90 % of the budget, lengthening the interval in whole multiples and reporting the stretch", () => {
+    const one = planCadence({ klinePollers: 1, pollMs: 1000, budgetPerMinute: 4000 });
+    expect(one).toEqual({ klineCadenceMs: 1000, stretched: false, projectedPerMinute: 121, fits: true });
+    const four = planCadence({ klinePollers: 4, pollMs: 1000, budgetPerMinute: 4000 });
+    expect(four).toEqual({ klineCadenceMs: 1000, stretched: false, projectedPerMinute: 481, fits: true });
+    const tight = planCadence({ klinePollers: 2, pollMs: 1000, budgetPerMinute: 100 });
+    expect(tight.klineCadenceMs).toBe(3000);
+    expect(tight.stretched).toBe(true);
+    expect(tight.projectedPerMinute).toBe(81);
+    expect(tight.fits).toBe(true);
+    const idle = planCadence({ klinePollers: 0, pollMs: 1000, budgetPerMinute: 4000 });
+    expect(idle).toEqual({ klineCadenceMs: 1000, stretched: false, projectedPerMinute: REQUEST_WEIGHTS.SERVER_TIME, fits: true });
+    expect(() => planCadence({ klinePollers: 1, pollMs: 0, budgetPerMinute: 4000 })).toThrow(/pollMs/);
+    expect(() => planCadence({ klinePollers: -1, pollMs: 1000, budgetPerMinute: 4000 })).toThrow(/klinePollers/);
+  });
+});

+  function restFakeFetch() {
+    return jest.fn(async (url) => {
+      const u = new URL(url);
+      if (u.pathname === "/api/v3/exchangeInfo" || u.pathname === "/api/v3/time") {
+        return jsonResponse({ serverTime: Date.now(), symbols: [] });
+      }
+      if (u.pathname === "/api/v3/klines") {
+        const startTime = Number(u.searchParams.get("startTime"));
+        const limit = Number(u.searchParams.get("limit"));
+        const currentOpen = Math.floor(Date.now() / MINUTE) * MINUTE;
+        const rows = [];
+        for (let openTime = startTime; openTime <= currentOpen && rows.length < limit; openTime += MINUTE) {
+          // The forming bar's close moves every second so the chart sees revisions.
+          const close = openTime === currentOpen ? `6427${Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) % 10}.00` : "64275.00";
+          rows.push([openTime, "64200.00", "64300.50", "64150.25", close, "12.3", openTime + MINUTE - 1, "792345.11", 418, "6.0", "385000.0", "0"]);
+        }
+        return jsonResponse(rows);
+      }
+      return new Response("{}", { status: 404 });
+    });
+  }
+

+      volumeProfileCachePath: ":memory:",
+      dxfeedBrokerUrl: "",
+    });
+    let client;
+    try {
+      await app.start();

+      expect(fetchImpl.mock.calls[0][0]).toBe("https://data-api.binance.vision/api/v3/exchangeInfo?symbol=BTCUSDT");
+      const port = app.httpServer.address().port;
+      const health = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/health`)).json();
+      expect(health.status).toEqual({ SPOT: { public: "CONNECTED" }, PERPETUAL: { public: "DISCONNECTED", market: "DISCONNECTED" } });
+      expect(health.delivery).toMatchObject({ transport: "rest", routeState: "CONNECTED", klineCadenceMs: 1000 });
+
+      client = new WebSocket(`ws://127.0.0.1:${port}/ws`);
+      const seen = [];
+      client.on("message", (data) => seen.push(JSON.parse(data.toString())));
+      const has = (type) => seen.some((frame) => frame.type === type);
+      const deadline = Date.now() + 8000;
+      while (!(has("health") && has("book") && has("bar")) && Date.now() < deadline) {
+        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
+      }

+        type: "health", provider: "BINANCE", symbol: "BTC-USDT", marketKind: "SPOT", recoveryState: "READY", stale: false,

+      });

+      expect(seen.filter((frame) => frame.type === "trade")).toEqual([]);

+      // gating is covered in the connection suite with the real resolver).

+### Binance REST transport (`TT_BINANCE_TRANSPORT=rest`)
+
+For a host that cannot open a WebSocket (a cloud container whose proxy refuses
+upgrades, a region Binance's primary API blocks). Spot only; set
+`TT_BINANCE_SPOT_REST_BASE_URL=https://data-api.binance.vision` beside it when
+`api.binance.com` is blocked. Nothing changes while the setting is absent.
+
+**What it serves: 1m candles**, from `/api/v3/klines`, each with open, high,
+low, close, volume, taker-buy volume (the sided candle flow) and trade count,
+forming and closed. Every `TT_BINANCE_REST_POLL_MS` the previous and the
+forming bar are read. Every row is classified by its own open and close time
+against the exchange clock as it stood when the request was sent, never by
+its position, by the machine clock or by the time of receipt: a bar is final
+only when the request left one second or more after its close time on the
+exchange (the exchange may build a row before the candle closes, so a row
+received after the close is kept forming until a later poll confirms it), and
+it is emitted closed exactly once; the forming bar is re-sent only when one of
+its seven fields changed; a response from an older poll never overwrites a
+newer one. The candle backfill applies the same rule to its pages. The
+exchange clock comes from the `exchangeInfo` probe that brings the route up
+(`serverTime`) and is re-read from `/api/v3/time` once a minute; a failed
+refresh keeps the last offset. After an outage of more than one bar the poller
+catches up from the last confirmed close, in pages of up to 1000 candles, at
+most 10 pages back to back before it yields for one cadence and continues; the
+health reason reads `catching up` until it is current. A page that starts past
+the next expected minute does not move the cursor: the exchange is asked again
+from the first missing minute, three times, with the reason still `catching
+up`; if it never supplies the interval, the cursor moves past it and the reason
+reads `history incomplete (N min missing)` for the rest of the session.
+
+**What it does not serve.** There is no tape and no order book. No trade frame
+is sent; the book is `UNAVAILABLE` with reason `REST_NO_DEPTH`. Unavailable:
+time and sales, Large Trade Bubbles and large-trade markers, aggressive-print
+clusters, every `ctx.ticks` and `ctx.trades` indicator (VWAP bands on prints,
+tick intensity), VPIN, the DOM, the Passive Liquidity heatmap and its walls,
+the L2 memory indicators (liquidity voids, L2 feed status), the Iceberg
+Detector, Exhaustion, Initiative Continuation, the `l2_*` channels and
+`bandHistory`. Works: candles and every resampled timeframe, `buy_volume` and
+`sell_volume` from taker-buy volume (`barSides`), `trade_count`, the candle
+backfill and Historical Mode candles, and every candle-only indicator (RSI,
+relative volume, structure, volatility, candle flow).
+
+**Health.** The feed's reason reads `Polled spot candles · no tape · no order
+book`, with `· rate limited` (a 429 or an IP ban: polling paused for
+`Retry-After`), `· poll failed` (the last poll failed or the route is down),
+`· catching up`, `· slow polling (weight budget)` or `· history incomplete (N
+min missing)` appended while they apply.
+The health frame's state follows the candle feed; the tape and depth feeds
+are reported unavailable and do not change that state. `/health` reports
+`delivery.transport: "rest"`, the cadence, the weight used and the exchange
+clock offset.
+
+**Weight.** The guarantee is this runtime's reserved dispatch budget: every
+request reserves its weight before it is sent (klines 2, `exchangeInfo` 20 for
+the connect probe and for each tick-size lookup, `time` 1, candle backfill 2
+per page), against `TT_BINANCE_REST_WEIGHT_BUDGET` per calendar minute
+(default 4000, at most 5000; the exchange limit is 6000). The exchange's own
+`x-mbx-used-weight-1m` raises the count when it is higher, and reservations
+keep adding on top of it. One gate holds every request, polls, clock refresh,
+backfill pages and tick-size lookups alike, during a rate-limit pause or an IP
+ban, and a 429 or 418 on any of them pauses all of them. The steady cadence plans on 90 % of the
+budget and is lengthened in whole multiples of the poll interval when the
+pairs would not fit; the reason then says `slow polling`. The budget is never
+exceeded to keep a cadence. One pair at 1 s costs about 121 of 4000 per minute.
+
+**Proxy.** Node's built-in `fetch` ignores `HTTPS_PROXY` unless
+`NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1` is set (Node 22.21 and later; the project pins 24.21.0).
+A custom CA is read from `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`, not from `SSL_CERT_FILE`. The
+stream transport (`ws`) never used a proxy and still does not.
+

const { MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST } = require("./binance-http-client");

const DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS = 1000;
const TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT = 2;
// A page that starts past the continuation cursor is asked for again this
// many times before the missing minutes are given up as not supplied.
const DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES = 3;

/**
 * The cursor over one pair's candles polled from /api/v3/klines. Every row is
 * classified by its own open and close time against the exchange clock as it
 * stood when the request was DISPATCHED, never by its position in the
 * response, by the machine clock or by the time of receipt: the exchange may
 * have built a row before the candle closed, so a row counts as final only
 * when the request left after that candle's close plus a grace; otherwise it
 * is the forming bar, re-sent as a revision whenever its values changed, and
 * a later poll confirms it. A response from an older poll never overwrites a
 * newer one. A page that starts beyond the next expected minute leaves the
 * continuation cursor on the first missing minute while the exchange is asked
 * again; after bounded retries the interval is recorded as missing and the
 * cursor moves past it, so the gap is reported rather than silently skipped.
 */
function createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs, finalizeGraceMs = DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS, maxHoleRetries = DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES } = {}) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(intervalMs) || intervalMs <= 0) throw new Error("intervalMs must be a positive safe integer");
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(finalizeGraceMs) || finalizeGraceMs < 0) throw new Error("finalizeGraceMs must be a non-negative safe integer");
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maxHoleRetries) || maxHoleRetries <= 0) throw new Error("maxHoleRetries must be a positive safe integer");

  let confirmedOpenTime = null;
  let lastSeq = 0;
  let holes = 0;
  let holeRetries = 0;
  let repairing = null; // { fromOpenTime, toOpenTime } while the exchange is asked again for missing minutes
  const missing = [];
  const forming = new Map();

  function currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) {
    return Math.floor(exchangeNowMs / intervalMs) * intervalMs;
  }

  /** After an outage the previous closed bar lies more than one bar past the last confirmed close. */
  function needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) {
    return confirmedOpenTime !== null && currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs > confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs;
  }

  function nextRequest(exchangeNowMs) {
    if (needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null) {
      return Object.freeze({ startTime: confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs, limit: MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST, catchUp: true });
    }
    return Object.freeze({ startTime: currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs, limit: TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT, catchUp: false });
  }

  function signatureOf(row) {
    return `${row.open}|${row.high}|${row.low}|${row.close}|${row.volume}|${row.numberOfTrades}|${row.takerBuyBaseVolume}`;
  }

  function outcome(fields, exchangeNowMs) {
    return Object.freeze({
      stale: false,
      emit: Object.freeze([]),
      hole: null,
      abandoned: null,
      repairing: repairing !== null,
      catchingUp: needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null,
      ...fields,
    });
  }

  /**
   * `rows` are parsed kline rows ascending by open time (parseKlineRows);
   * `exchangeNowMs` is the exchange time when their request was dispatched;
   * `seq` orders the polls that produced them.
   */
  function apply({ rows, exchangeNowMs, seq }) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(seq) || seq <= lastSeq) {
      return outcome({ stale: true }, exchangeNowMs);
    }
    lastSeq = seq;
    const pending = rows.filter((row) => confirmedOpenTime === null || row.openTime > confirmedOpenTime);
    let hole = null;
    let abandoned = null;
    if (pending.length > 0 && confirmedOpenTime !== null && pending[0].openTime > confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs) {
      hole = Object.freeze({ fromOpenTime: confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs, toOpenTime: pending[0].openTime - intervalMs });
      if (repairing === null || repairing.fromOpenTime !== hole.fromOpenTime) {
        holes += 1;
        holeRetries = 0;
        repairing = hole;
      }
      holeRetries += 1;
      if (holeRetries < maxHoleRetries) {
        // Ask again from the first missing minute before believing the gap.
        return outcome({ hole }, exchangeNowMs);
      }
      // The exchange did not supply the interval: record it as missing and
      // move the cursor past it so the candles it does have are delivered.
      abandoned = hole;
      missing.push(hole);
      repairing = null;
      holeRetries = 0;
      confirmedOpenTime = hole.toOpenTime;
    } else if (pending.length > 0) {
      repairing = null;
      holeRetries = 0;
    }
    const emit = [];
    for (const row of pending) {
      const closed = row.closeTime + finalizeGraceMs <= exchangeNowMs;
      if (closed) {
        emit.push(Object.freeze({ row, closed: true }));
        confirmedOpenTime = row.openTime;
        forming.delete(row.openTime);
        continue;
      }
      const signature = signatureOf(row);
      if (forming.get(row.openTime) === signature) continue;
      forming.set(row.openTime, signature);
      emit.push(Object.freeze({ row, closed: false }));
    }
    for (const openTime of forming.keys()) {
      if (confirmedOpenTime !== null && openTime <= confirmedOpenTime) forming.delete(openTime);
    }
    return outcome({ emit: Object.freeze(emit), hole, abandoned }, exchangeNowMs);
  }

  function reset() {
    confirmedOpenTime = null;
    repairing = null;
    holeRetries = 0;
    forming.clear();
  }

  /** Minutes the exchange never supplied, summed over the recorded missing intervals. */
  function missingBars() {
    return missing.reduce((sum, gap) => sum + (gap.toOpenTime - gap.fromOpenTime) / intervalMs + 1, 0);
  }

  return Object.freeze({
    nextRequest,
    needsCatchUp,
    apply,
    reset,
    get confirmedOpenTime() { return confirmedOpenTime; },
    get repairing() { return repairing; },
    get missing() { return Object.freeze([...missing]); },
    missingBars,
    get stats() { return Object.freeze({ confirmedOpenTime, holes, holeRetries, lastSeq, missingBars: missingBars() }); },
  });
}

module.exports = { createBinanceRestBarCursor, DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS, DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES, TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT };
