      '  /** After an outage the previous closed bar lies more than one bar past the last confirmed close. */\n' +
      '  function needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) {\n' +
      '    return confirmedOpenTime !== null && currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs > confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs;\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '\n' +
      '  function nextRequest(exchangeNowMs) {\n' +
      '    if (needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null) {\n' +
      '      return Object.freeze({ startTime: confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs, limit: MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST, catchUp: true });\n' +
      '    }\n' +
      '    return Object.freeze({ startTime: currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs, limit: TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT, catchUp: false });\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '\n' +
      '  function signatureOf(row) {\n' +
      '    return `${row.open}|${row.high}|${row.low}|${row.close}|${row.volume}|${row.numberOfTrades}|${row.takerBuyBaseVolume}`;\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '\n' +
      '  function outcome(fields, exchangeNowMs) {\n' +
      '    return Object.freeze({\n' +
      '      stale: false,\n' +
      '      emit: Object.freeze([]),\n' +
      '      hole: null,\n' +
      '      abandoned: null,\n' +
      '      repairing: repairing !== null,\n' +
      '      catchingUp: needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null,\n' +
      '      ...fields,\n' +
      '    });\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '\n' +
      '  /**\n' +
      '   * `rows` are parsed kline rows ascending by open time (parseKlineRows);\n' +
      '   * `exchangeNowMs` is the exchange time when their request was dispatched;\n' +
      '   * `seq` orders the polls that produced them.\n' +
      '   */\n' +
      '  function apply({ rows, exchangeNowMs, seq }) {\n' +
      '    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(seq) || seq <= lastSeq) {\n' +
      '      return outcome({ stale: true }, exchangeNowMs);\n' +
      '    }\n' +
      '    lastSeq = seq;\n' +
      '    const pending = rows.filter((row) => confirmedOpenTime === null || row.openTime > confirmedOpenTime);\n' +
      '    let hole = null;\n' +
      '    let abandoned = null;\n' +
      '    if (pending.length > 0 && confirmedOpenTime !== null && pending[0].openTime > confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs) {\n' +
      '      hole = Object.freeze({ fromOpenTime: confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs, toOpenTime: pending[0].openTime - intervalMs });\n' +
      '      if (repairing === null || repairing.fromOpenTime !== hole.fromOpenTime) {\n' +
      '        holes += 1;\n' +
      '        holeRetries = 0;\n' +
      '        repairing = hole;\n' +
      '      }\n' +
      '      holeRetries += 1;\n' +
      '      if (holeRetries < maxHoleRetries) {\n' +
      '        // Ask again from the first missing minute before believing the gap.\n' +
      '        return outcome({ hole }, exchangeNowMs);\n' +
      '      }\n' +
      '      // The exchange did not supply the interval: record it as missing and\n' +
      '      // move the cursor past it so the candles it does have are delivered.\n' +
      '      abandoned = hole;\n' +
      '      missing.push(hole);\n' +
      '      repairing = null;\n' +
      '      holeRetries = 0;\n' +
      '      confirmedOpenTime = hole.toOpenTime;\n' +
      '    } else if (pending.length > 0) {\n' +
      '      repairing = null;\n' +
      '      holeRetries = 0;\n' +
      '    }\n' +
      '    const emit = [];\n' +
      '    for (const row of pending) {\n' +
      '      const closed = row.closeTime + finalizeGraceMs <= exchangeNowMs;\n' +
      '      if (closed) {\n' +
      '        emit.push(Object.freeze({ row, closed: true }));\n' +
      '        confirmedOpenTime = row.openTime;\n' +
      '        forming.delete(row.openTime);\n' +
      '        continue;\n' +
      '      }\n' +
      '      const signature = signatureOf(row);\n' +
      '      if (forming.get(row.openTime) === signature) continue;\n' +
      '      forming.set(row.openTime, signature);\n' +
      '      emit.push(Object.freeze({ row, closed: false }));\n' +
      '    }\n' +
      '    for (const openTime of forming.keys()) {\n' +
      '      if (confirmedOpenTime !== null && openTime <= confirmedOpenTime) forming.delete(openTime);\n' +
      '    }\n' +
      '    return outcome({ emit: Object.freeze(emit), hole, abandoned }, exchangeNowMs);\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '\n' +
      '  function reset() {\n' +
      '    confirmedOpenTime = null;\n' +
      '    repairing = null;\n' +
      '    holeRetries = 0;\n' +
      '    forming.clear();\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '\n' +
      '  /** Minutes the exchange never supplied, summed over the recorded missing intervals. */\n' +
      '  function missingBars() {\n' +
      '    return missing.reduce((sum, gap) => sum + (gap.toOpenTime - gap.fromOpenTime) / intervalMs + 1, 0);\n' +
      '  }\n' +
      '\n' +
      '  return Object.freeze({\n' +
      '    nextRequest,\n' +
      '    needsCatchUp,\n' +
      '    apply,\n' +
      '    reset,\n' +
      '    get confirmedOpenTime() { return confirmedOpenTime; },\n' +
      '    get repairing() { return repairing; },\n' +
      '    get missing() { return Object.freeze([...missing]); },\n' +
      '    missingBars,\n' +
      '    get stats() { return Object.freeze({ confirmedOpenTime, holes, holeRetries, lastSeq, missingBars: missingBars() }); },\n' +
      '  });\n' +
      '}\n' +
      '\n' +
      'module.exports = { createBinanceRestBarCursor, DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS, DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES, TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT };\n',
    ''
  ],
  pid: 10,
  stdout: 'const { MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST } = require("./binance-http-client");\n' +
    '\n' +
    'const DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS = 1000;\n' +
    'const TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT = 2;\n' +
    '// A page that starts past the continuation cursor is asked for again this\n' +
    '// many times before the missing minutes are given up as not supplied.\n' +
    'const DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES = 3;\n' +
    '\n' +
    '/**\n' +
    " * The cursor over one pair's candles polled from /api/v3/klines. Every row is\n" +
    ' * classified by its own open and close time against the exchange clock as it\n' +
    ' * stood when the request was DISPATCHED, never by its position in the\n' +
    ' * response, by the machine clock or by the time of receipt: the exchange may\n' +
    ' * have built a row before the candle closed, so a row counts as final only\n' +
    " * when the request left after that candle's close plus a grace; otherwise it\n" +
    ' * is the forming bar, re-sent as a revision whenever its values changed, and\n' +
    ' * a later poll confirms it. A response from an older poll never overwrites a\n' +
    ' * newer one. A page that starts beyond the next expected minute leaves the\n' +
    ' * continuation cursor on the first missing minute while the exchange is asked\n' +
    ' * again; after bounded retries the interval is recorded as missing and the\n' +
    ' * cursor moves past it, so the gap is reported rather than silently skipped.\n' +
    ' */\n' +
    'function createBinanceRestBarCursor({ intervalMs, finalizeGraceMs = DEFAULT_FINALIZE_GRACE_MS, maxHoleRetries = DEFAULT_MAX_HOLE_RETRIES } = {}) {\n' +
    '  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(intervalMs) || intervalMs <= 0) throw new Error("intervalMs must be a positive safe integer");\n' +
    '  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(finalizeGraceMs) || finalizeGraceMs < 0) throw new Error("finalizeGraceMs must be a non-negative safe integer");\n' +
    '  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maxHoleRetries) || maxHoleRetries <= 0) throw new Error("maxHoleRetries must be a positive safe integer");\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  let confirmedOpenTime = null;\n' +
    '  let lastSeq = 0;\n' +
    '  let holes = 0;\n' +
    '  let holeRetries = 0;\n' +
    '  let repairing = null; // { fromOpenTime, toOpenTime } while the exchange is asked again for missing minutes\n' +
    '  const missing = [];\n' +
    '  const forming = new Map();\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  function currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) {\n' +
    '    return Math.floor(exchangeNowMs / intervalMs) * intervalMs;\n' +
    '  }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  /** After an outage the previous closed bar lies more than one bar past the last confirmed close. */\n' +
    '  function needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) {\n' +
    '    return confirmedOpenTime !== null && currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs > confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs;\n' +
    '  }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  function nextRequest(exchangeNowMs) {\n' +
    '    if (needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null) {\n' +
    '      return Object.freeze({ startTime: confirmedOpenTime + intervalMs, limit: MAX_KLINES_PER_REQUEST, catchUp: true });\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    return Object.freeze({ startTime: currentOpen(exchangeNowMs) - intervalMs, limit: TWO_ROW_POLL_LIMIT, catchUp: false });\n' +
    '  }\n' +
    '\n' +
    '  function signatureOf(row) {\n' +
    '    return `${row.open}|${row.high}|${row.low}|${row.close}|${row.volume}|${row.numberOfTrades}|${row.takerBuyBaseVolume}`;\n' +

    '\n' +

    '      catchingUp: needsCatchUp(exchangeNowMs) || repairing !== null,\n' +
    '      ...fields,\n' +

    '  }\n' +
    '\n' +

    '   * `exchangeNowMs` is the exchange time when their request was dispatched;\n' +
    '   * `seq` orders the polls that produced them.\n' +
    '   */\n' +
    '  function apply({ rows, exchangeNowMs, seq }) {\n' +
    '    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(seq) || seq <= lastSeq) {\n' +
    '      return outcome({ stale: true }, exchangeNowMs);\n' +
    '    }\n' +
    '    lastSeq = seq;\n' +

codex
I reproduced a cursor bug: ten polls at one-minute intervals, each 500 ms after the minute boundary, emit no closed candles. Before any close is confirmed, the cursor also cannot catch up after an outage. I found a separate health issue: an empty candle response can leave the feed reporting READY despite receiving no candle data.

codex
1. **WARNING — `apps/runtime/src/providers/binance/binance-rest-bar-cursor.js`, lines 40–49:** Catch-up requires an already-confirmed close. If subscription starts inside the finalisation grace and polling then pauses, missed candles are skipped because `confirmedOpenTime` remains null. With the permitted `pollMs=60000`, polling consistently at `:00.500` never finalises any candle; the forming map also grows indefinitely. An executable reproduction produced zero closed candles after ten polls.
   
   **Fix:** Track the earliest unconfirmed minute from the first request and continue from it until finalised or explicitly marked missing. Add tests for an outage before the first confirmed close and minute-long polling inside the grace window.

2. **WARNING — `apps/runtime/src/providers/binance/binance-rest-connection.js`, lines 576–579 and 635–638:** Empty kline responses refresh feed freshness and can end recovery. At startup, `rows: []` produces `catchingUp: false`, so repeated empty responses report READY indefinitely without delivering candles. Responses containing only obsolete rows can similarly hide a stalled candle feed.
   
   **Fix:** Distinguish HTTP success from candle coverage. Keep recovery active when expected minutes are absent, retain the continuation cursor, and base candle freshness on relevant observations. Add tests for empty startup responses, repeated empty polls, and responses containing only old candles.

Reviewed against the final candles-only amendment. Cursor reproductions ran directly against branch source; the full Jest suite was not run because the read-only workspace is checked out on `main`.

VERDICT: REVISE
